JU/NE=WI Speculations. Pretended luck. General carelessness.
#
WI/JU=PL Fortunate development in the public.
WI/PL=JU Changes take place in monetary matters. Fortunate developments.
JU/PL=WI Fortunate transformations and changes in general.
#
WI/JU=CU Happiness in married life.
WI/CU=JU Banks and financial institutions.
JU/CU=WI To find oneself happily related to the public. Contented body of artists.
#
WI/JU=HA Fortune in misfortune.
WI/HA=JU Tropical fevers. Liver complaints. Money losses because of a catastrophe. Dirty financial transactions.
JU/HA=WI General money shortage. Inflation, swindle with money. General worries about money conditions.
#
WI/JU=ZE Fortunate creation. The happiness in one's work.
WI/ZE=JU To produce coins. To create something valuable. To be generally engaged in successful work projects.
JU/ZE=WI General prosperous working and accomplishing. Products. Creations.
#
WI/JU=KR Happiness through the father, superiors or authority figures.
WI/KR=JU The successful leader. To enjoy good receipts, growth in income. Dealing with financial transactions arid banks.
JU/KR=WI Financial giants.
#
WI/JU=AP Luck and increase in commerce and industry. Expanded fortune. Fortunate masses of people.
WI/AP=JU The science of economy. The economist. Great financial institutions. Large amounts of money. To gather an extensive fortune. Several fortunate happenings at the same time.
JU/AP=WI To be happy with many. Expansion of good fortune in general.
#
WI/JU=AD General rest and quiet. Rich sources of raw materials. Gains from raw materials.
WI/AD=JU Moderate luck. Fortunate in retirement. A fortune is made in the acquisition of property. The overcoming of difficulties in general.
JU/AD=WI Rest and seclusion. Luck in general is confined. Production of raw materials.
#
WI/JU=VU Great success in general ... optimism.
WI/VU=JU Financial power. Power through possessions. To accumulate a great fortune.
JU/VU=WI Optimism. General confidence.
#
WI/JU=PO One's common sense or reason.
WI/PO=JU Fortunate understanding, reason, wisdom.
JU/PO=WI The general reason and intelligence. The general law conditions. World conscience.
#
WI/SA=UR Sudden parting from another person. Sudden death.
WI/UR=SA Events connected with a separation.
SA/UR=WI General leave-taking. Sudden events with property losses.
#
WI/SA=NE Separation through sickness. Suspected infection. Quarantine. To put a stop to a state.
WI/NE=SA The end of a romantic affair ... of a beautiful dream. Losses through fraud. Pestilential diseases.
SA/NE=WI General plagues. Serious sickness in the family.
#
WI/SA=PL Matters come to a standstill. Slow, hindered developments. Beginning of a separation.
WI/PL=SA The development is slowed-up, hindered or retarded.
SA/PL=WI The general public cannot adapt and separates from a development, and only gets used to new conditions very slowly.
#
WI/SA=CU Divorce.
WI/CU=SA Separation from groups, companies or associations. Divorces in the family.
SA/CU=WI General common connections are separated. Divorces.
#
WI/SA=HA Dismissals of many workers. Epidemics. To move away for sad reasons. Refugees. To be a fugitive.
WI/HA=SA Poor pitied humans. Refugees and strangers. Decay. Not able to identify after death.
SA/HA=WI Sad parting. Fugitives. Famine. Scarcity o, employment. Damage through frost. Severe damage or plagues.
#
WI/SA=ZE Estate or home lost by fire. To work under heavy restrictions and disagreeableness.
WI/ZE=SA Losses by fire. Activity accompanied by great losses.
SA/ZE=WI General damages through steam power, and losses through fire.
#
WI/SA=KR Lockouts, hindrances through officials or superiors. Shutout, lockout, territorial losses.
WI/KR=SA Forestry. Large agricultural estates. Separation and prohibition ordered by the state. Authorities issue orders to evacuate.
SA/KR=WI Poor government structures.
#
WI/SA=AP Restrictions in commerce. Separation of the masses. Mass separations. Matters of education. The teacher.
WI/AP=SA Pedagogy (the art, profession or study of teaching). Agriculture. To teach or be a student. This axis can also indicate hindrances when other negative patterns are present.
SA/AP=WI Many separate. To separate from many or from the generality.
#
WI/SA=AD To be urged into a corner. General restriction and curbing. Obstruction and standstill.
WI/AD=SA Endurance. Patience. Chronic resistance (consistently). Losses.
SA/AD=WI General shrinkage, hindrance and lack.
#
WI/SA=VU To cordon off. A state of emergency. The state of siege. General obstruction and hindrance. To be watched over.
WI/VU=SA To shadow someone or something (follow secretly). To watch, to guard, to supervise. The supervisor.
SA/VU=WI The people in general are subject to strong hindrances and obstacles and therefore have to watch their chances or advantages.
#
WI/SA=PO Public education or educator.
WI/PO=SA Education, pupils.
SA/PO=WI Mental, spiritual or ideological separation from the generality. General affairs of education.
#
WI/UR=NE From here to the beyond. To make connection with the dead. To die.
WI/NE=UR Sudden losses of a general character. From the beyond to here.
UR/NE=WI Metaphysics. Occultism. Funerals. Cemeteries.
#
WI/UR=PL Developing by leaps and bounds. Mutation.
WI/PL=UR A surprising situation suddenly develops. Technical changes.
UR/PL=WI The generality suddenly faces changed conditions which must adapt itself to them.
#
WI/UR=CU To marry suddenly.
WI/CU=UR Unexpected sudden events in a corporation or community.
UR/CU=WI A quickly contracted marriage. Communities and/or partnerships which are surprisingly found together.
#
WI/UR=HA Sorrow through a sudden event. Serious accidents, attack, murders.
WI/HA=UR Negligence resulting in loss of life.
UR/HA=WI Unexpected events are taking a toll of life. Sudden murder.
#
WI/UR=ZE Thunderstorms. Danger of fire. Explosions.
WI/ZE=UR Fire spitting craters. Lightening. Manufacturing explosives. Spontaneous work in general.
UR/ZE=WI Fierce acts of violence. Storms. Eruptions. The general public is subjected to force, is tense and directed.
#
WI/UR=KR Surprising ordinances of the state. Events in a state or nation. Coup d' etat.
WI/KR=UR Technical establishments. Sudden events in ruling circles. Very extensive excitement and tension.
UR/KR=WI Excitement in ruling circles. Force of nature. Impetuous rise. Despot. To get excited through ordinances of the authorities. Days of great excitement.
#
WI/UR=AP Sudden events for many. Astrology.
WI/AP=UR Extended reforms. New branches of science, Sudden expansion. Revolution in science. An event that causes mass excitement.
UR/AP=WI The generality, in expectation of coming things, waits with tenseness for the decisions.
#
WI/UR=AD Surprising destruction.
WI/AD=UR Sudden heavy depression. Excitement, aggravations through obstacles or a turbulent nature. Wreckage, broken pieces and rags. Surprised by the sudden death of another person.
UR/AD=WI To be separated from the general public through such violent events. The general public is under the impression of violent commotions or events.
#
WI/UR=VU Suddenly appearing force. Electrical energies.
WI/VU=UR Explosive power. Combustion in a motor. A suddenly appearing force.
UR/VU=WI To be connected with violent acts in public. Kinetic powers (dynamics of motion). Electric current.
#
WI/UR=PO Sudden appearance of a new idea. Propaganda.
WI/PO=UR Enlightenment, sudden faculty of perception, truth.
UR/PO=WI Mental alertness in general. To carry out propaganda. Eager expectations of the general public, brought into motion through ideas. Motion pictures. Enlightenment.
#
WI/NE=PL Changes below the surface. Unnoticeable general transformations.
WI/PL=NE It is difficult to say in what direction the situation will go. Indeterminate as to direction.
NE/PL=WI Changes in general which cannot be estimated correctly.
#
WI/NE=CU Precarious marital conditions. Dormant art. Dissolution of unions.
WI/CU=NE Collapse of a corporation. Fraudulent banks or other institutions, Suppressed organizations.
NE/CU=WI Dissolution of a family. General dissatisfaction with marital or communal institutions. Dissolution of a community with many. To move away.
#
WI/NE=HA Sorrow and discomfort through uncertainties. Serious blood ailments. Want of water. Rough to stormi weather. Water catastrophes, floods.
WI/HA=NE Serious or fatal diseases.
NE/HA=WI The native deceives himself about his relations to the surroundings and the general world. Also the environment may not be sure how it feels about the native. Floods. Tidal waves.
#
WI/NE=ZE Restriction in activity through malice and hatefulness. Disruptions in the railroad business or machine establishments. To derange or mess up.
WI/ZE=NE Wasted energies. Heat vapors. Combustible gas. To reject an irresistible impulse. Fire fighting institutions.
NE/ZE=WI Exploding gases. Wrecked coal mines. Destroyed common property as, for example, by war. Defeats in war. Unsuccessful fight. Gas war.
#
WI/NE=KR Leading persons disappoint the general public. Diplomatic entanglements.
WI/KR=NE A very poor leader. Loss of authority. Loss of power. A government that is not recognized as such.
NE/KR=WI The general public lives under wrong impressions, is kept so the wrong impression is increased. Incompetence in ruling circles.
#
WI/NE=AP Far spread chaos. Corruption. Dissolutions. Disappointment of the masses.
WI/AP=NE Extensive deception. Occult sciences. On the way to organized science.
NE/AP=WI Corruption. The general deception about the future. The general uncertainty about the near future.
#
WI/NE=AD Until now uncertain conditions are settled. Forming of ice. Icing up glaciation.
WI/AD=NE To solidify. To make solid. To embalm. To mummify. The embalmer. To preserve. Able to prevent decay, putrefying or decomposition. involved in the preservation of things.
NE/AD=WI To change the form of being in general. To enter new conditions of existence.
#
WI/NE=VU Great disappointments.
WI/VU=NE Disguise. Power felt and sensed, but not seen or heard. Power that lies in deception. The silent powers.
NE/VU=WI To live through a great disappointment with the general world or the general world is deceived by the native, or the reverse.
#
WI/NE=PO Fanaticism. Fanatic penetration of spiritual energies. Smoke and vapors.
WI/PO=NE Refined mental notions. Despondency. Deterioration of a philosophy or belief.
NE/PO=WI The general false faith. The generality has the wrong attitude. It succumbs to great errors.
#
WI/PL=CU Engagement, marriage, births and Tradition. Growth of families and communities. Inherited qualities. Changes in the perception of the arts.
WI/CU=PL Communities under construction. The development of corporations. Art develops.
PL/CU=WI Changing of the structure of human society. General social transformations.
#
WI/PL=HA To be guilty of a failure to act. General decline.
WI/HA=PL Increasing poverty and misfortune. In some cases, changes for the better. Increase in coal production.
PL/HA=WI Symptoms of deterioration and decadence. General decline. General break down.
#
WI/PL=ZE New weapons. The capacity for work increases.
WI/ZE=PL Increasing efficiency. The performance of duties. A transformation by natural forces.
PL/ZE=WI General eagerness to produce. Problems everyone has to face.
#
WI/PL=KR Changes taking place in the government or administration. General rise.
WI/KR=PL To develop eminent skills. Mast". Great transformations in the world.
PL/KR=WI Great changes and transformations in general.
#
WI/PL=AP Peaceful development. Growth in science, commerce and industry.
WI/AP=PL Comprehensive transformations in the field of world science. The development of commerce and science. Changes.
PL/AP=WI A general transformation or change which starts from small beginnings. A changed attitude towards people in general. To set a precedent. Test case.
#
WI/PL=AD Deep reaching changes from the bottom up.
WI/AD=PL Deep reaching changes in the world.
PL/AD=WI Changed general conditions affect the native. Making the best of it.
#
WI/PL=VU Stormi arid fevered development.
WI/VU=PL A forced change or transformation.
PL/VU=WI To experience great changes in general. Great general transformation. To experience a change.
#
WI/PL=PO Developing new ideas. General spiritual transformations.
WI/PO=PL The general mental or spiritual development and transformation in the world.
PL/PO=WI General transformations of religious or of world concept.
#
WI/CU=HA Collapse of corporations. Business depressions.
WI/HA=CU Miserable marriage conditions. General low brow art ... popular art.
CU/HA=WI Indigent populace. Stock companies which have a hard struggle.
#
WI/CU=ZE The founding of a corporation or club.
WI/ZE=CU An organization with a goal such as trade-unions or political parties. A general increase in the number of marriages.
CU/ZE=WI To establish new societies (corporations). General artistic products. Comrade in arms.
#
WI/CU=KR A great financial institution such as a national bank. A lucrative stock company. Distinguished society. To maintain the highest artistic standard.
WI/KR=CU A manager. An international art authority.
CU/KR=WI To demonstrate or exhibit one's abilities. Artistic activity.
#
WI/CU=AP Widespread associations in science, commerce and industry. A major artistic accomplishment.
WI/AP=CU Extensive efforts for peace. Societies for peace. The science of the arts. Expansion in great organizations.
CU/AP=WI General peaceful efforts. General scientific or artistic associations. To become known in wide circles through art or science.
#
WI/CU=AD Celibacy. Exclusive community or social circle. A private party.
WI/AD=CU Dead arts. Deaths in the family. A small company. The property that belongs to a company. Isolation.
CU/AD=WI To get into strange surroundings, to be without connections. To feel lost.
#
WI/CU=VU A political party. A mighty influential community.
WI/VU=CU Power pressure exerted by an association, community or political party.
CU/VU=WI To become generally known through art creation. Mighty general art creation. Powerful organizations.
#
WI/CU=PO The church. A religious sect. A religious congregation.
WI/PO=CU The refined mental and religious attitude in marriage, community or the art world. Religious communities. Societies based on a common perception of the world.
CU/PO=WI General religious communions, confessions, sects, churches.
#
WI/HA=ZE Coal fire (serviceable). Incinerators, crematoriums. Unemployment.
WI/ZE=HA Meanness and dirty tricks prevent one from reaching a goal. Devastation by open firs. (forest fires etc.). Fires started by lightning.
HA/ZE=WI Malice, dirty tricks. Strike. Lock-outs. Lack of coal.
#
WI/HA=KR Great general want. Great depression. General worries through leading forces. Coal mining.
WI/KR=HA Great misfortune and poverty for many people.
HA/KR=WI Atrocities. Great mortality. Sacrifices of human lives. Great crimes of or against the government. Failure of a plot.
#
WI/HA=AP Widespread poverty. Far expanded coal fields. Coal-trading.
WI/AP=HA The science of history. Dealers in junk or waste. Widespread deficiencies.
HA/AP=WI General poverty and general misery. Dealing with rags. Second hand clothing. The attitude toward the past.
#
WI/HA=AD Lack of raw material. Junk materials and waste.
WI/AD=HA The beginning of the end of a disagreeable situation. Gaining new raw materials from junk. New raw materials from the earth or from coal etc.
HA/AD=WI General lack of raw materials. Sagging economies, sagging living standards. General pessimism.
#
WI/HA=VU Ugliness and dangerous plagues. Capital crimes.
WI/VU=HA The pressure of meanness, lowness, vulgarity.
HA/VU=WI General dependence and suppression. Vices, obstacles, and dirt. General ugly manners and maneuvers. Raw and uncultured people. Terrible acts of violence in public life.
#
WI/HA=PO Spiritual poverty and ignorance. Want and need lead to knowledge and cognition.
WI/PO=HA The spirit of antiquity or the past. Mental or spiritual baseness.
HA/PO=WI Mental blindness and poverty in general. To deal generally with people where no mental contact can be made.
#
WI/ZE=KR To be one's own boss (self-employed). War apparatus. Wars brought about by the government.
WI/KR=ZE Extraordinary accomplishments. To achieve something great. A large fire.
ZE/KR=WI The general energetic will in pursuit of a goal. Big fires. Wars. Deciding battles.
#
WI/ZE=AP Natural science. Expansion. The situation spreads out and expands by way of a compelling force.
WI/AP=ZE A technical institute. Building of engines. The general field of scientific creativity.
ZE/AP=WI The people in general try to find a solution to present problems by practical application. General problems demanding a solution.
#
WI/ZE=AD Earthquake. Eruption of the earth. A difficult problem is attacked.
WI/AD=ZE To start to tackle a difficult job. Fire in the earth such as lava from a volcano.
ZE/AD=WI To face new problems in general, a new beginning, new environment, new circumstances.
#
WI/ZE=VU Extraordinary natural forces at work. Powerful machine energies. To utilize natural forces.
WI/VU=ZE Great fires. Directed and guided forces. Immense creations.
ZE/VU=WI Great urge in general to accomplish things.
#
WI/ZE=PO To express logic. Proofs. Work involves the creative intellectual united with the higher mind. Spiritual work or creativity.
WI/PO=ZE Proofs. Mental activity. Mental leadership.
ZE/PO=WI Mental disposition. To be under a general mental leadership. To be mentally influenced and impressed by others. The generality must witness something, must prove something or must show its conviction.
#
WI/KR=AP Mass expansion. Scientific authorities.
WI/AP=KR One who is a scientific authority. Great masses of people.
KR/AP=WI The rising of the authority in public. Things which are expanding. Growing authoritative attitude in public. The public understands a matter.
#
WI/KR=AD The overthrow of a government. One who is an authority for a few. Mountains. Great altitudes around the world.
WI/AD=KR Low ground, the valley. Great cracking, dividing, splitting. An authority for a few people. Deaths or difficulties among people in authority.
KR/AD=WI To withdraw from the generality. Men who join together to live in an odd way, who live for the same idea.
#
WI/KR=VU Powerful nations, very great forces at work, and great powers.
WI/VU=KR Exercising executive power of a state or country. The great powers. Using the influence of one's high position.
KR/VU=WI Great powers. Powers of States. To be under the impression or influence of the power of the State.
#
WI/KR=PO Philanthropy. The dignity of men. The love of mankind. Magnitude of intellect and spirit. Very high culture.
WI/PO=KR Mental or intellectual leadership. Spiritual standard of the time.
KR/PO=WI Mental giants in general. The high mental or spiritual influence in the public. The general spirituality, the level or standard of civilisation or culture for the time being. General cultural efforts.
#
WI/AP=AD Stagnation. A general standstill. Stoppage.
WI/AD=AP Finding extensive raw materials. General difficulties in commerce and trade. Limited success. Dying of the masses.
AP/AD=WI General tranquility. To be carefree. General contentment.
#
WI/AP=VU Enormous expansion and spreading out. Very extraordinary successes.
WI/VU=AP Commercial power. Great expansion and success. Power of trade. Wholesale merchants. Far reaching power and influence in the world. The power of science put into play.
AP/VU=WI Great general success. Prosperity and wealth in general. The expanding power of prosperity.
#
WI/AP=PO Companions, friends - fellow sufferers. Widely spreading ideas in the world.
WI/PO=AP The general mental or spiritual freedom in the world, country or community.
AP/PO=WI The dominating disposition (view). The general mental and emotional attitude.
#
WI/AD=VU To bundle or to compress something. obstacles through the present powers.
WI/VU=AD Stability. Perseverance. Limitation and derogation (repeal, distract, restrict) of power and influence. Mighty opposition and resistance.
AD/VU=WI The general public, treated harshly by fate, must show firmness and power of resistance. Give and take the hard way with reference to the general public.
#
WI/AD=PO Education, development, culture, training. General mental obstacles as well.
WI/PO=AD The condition of education and culture in the world, country or community.
AD/PO=WI The general educational facilities.
#
WI/VU=PO Mental power and very extensive mental influence in the world.
WI/PO=VU Powerful ideas that have an extremely powerful influence.
VU/PO=WI The general state of mind, ruling ideas, affecting everybody.
#
MC/AS=SO To have close professional contact with others. Working together.
MC/SO=AS The mental and professional attitude toward others.
AS/SO=MC Personal conduct toward other people. The impression made by the personal environment on the native.
#
MC/AS=MO One's behavior toward women. Professional relations to the public and women.
MC/MO=AS The psychological influence of women in the environment. One's mental attitude toward others.
AS/MO=MC Concerning one's own mother. Acquaintances with women. The psychological influence of a woman. To be brought into public by others.
#
MC/AS=KN To become familiar. Professional associations become personal where there is a common interest.
MC/KN=AS Soul unions in the personal environment.
AS/KN=MC The attitude of the soul in relation to others and the closer surroundings. Many personal contacts with acquaintances. Close personal contact.
#
MC/AS=ME Talking about the occupation. Vocational or business conversations.
MC/ME=AS To discuss another person. To speak with others. The opinion one has about another person.
AS/ME=MC The opinion of others about the native. To get news and information. To converse with others.
#
MC/AS=VE Agreeable professional connections. To be content with professional relations. Harmonious associations in the near surroundings.
MC/VE=AS To be in love with someone. To show a preference for certain places. To be located at that place or to long for that place.
AS/VE=MC To be happy and glad about the treatment we receive from others. Love's influence upon the mind.
#
MC/AS=MA One's professional activities. To develop or accomplish something with others.
MC/MA=AS One who is active with other people. Giving orders to others.
AS/MA=MC Others act upon or with me. To make common cause with others.
#
MC/AS=JU To have professional luck. To enjoy oneself with others.
MC/JU=AS The desire to make others happy. To be jolly with others. To be content and in harmony with others.
AS/JU=MC To get along well with others. To keep good friendship. To be fond of companionship. To have protection. To be protected by others.
#
MC/AS=SA To have professional difficulties. To separate from others.
MC/SA=AS To share other people's grief. To separate from others.
AS/SA=MC To be overlooked or to suffer setbacks through others. Estrangement. Others leave. The mental influence of separations.
#
MC/AS=UR To find oneself suddenly facing others. Experiencing professional trouble. To make a scene.
MC/UR=AS To be excited over others.
AS/UR=MC To be excited by or over other people.
#
MC/AS=NE To be deceived by others. Experiencing deceptions with others. Unstable professional associates.
MC/NE=AS To mislead others. To commit frauds with other people.
AS/NE=MC To suffer from fraudulent deeds of others.
#
MC/AS=PL Developments or changes in the professional life or in relation to others. Professional changes effect the native's personality.
MC/PL=AS To effect the development of others, or to influence the same.
AS/PL=MC The influence by others on the native. Changes at the place interfere with the development of the native.
#
MC/AS=CU To be active in society. To conclude a marriage. Having professional associates.
MC/CU=AS Relationships with art-lovers. With others in an association.
AS/CU=MC One's own mental attitude toward the apartment, the home or the home of the parents.
#
MC/AS=HA To be in a sad mood with others. To be connected with ugly and dirty things.
MC/HA=AS Saddened by the attitude of the environment. To share other people's worries and sorrows.
AS/HA=MC Psychic influence through the poverty of others. To be disgusted with the surroundings.
#
MC/AS=ZE To incite others. Skilled at inspiring enthusiasm. Compelling directives.
MC/ZE=AS Teaching. To help others intellectually. To force one's will on others. Instructions.
AS/ZE=MC To be incited to an act by others.
#
MC/AS=KR To be bossy and haughty. Self-confident. Independent professionals.
MC/KR=AS To lead others. Connections with inferiors.
AS/KR=MC To be a workman, employee, official. inability to occupy an independent position in life. To serve the boss with body and soul. Servility.
#
MC/AS=AP Professional success. Dealings with many people. Reaching the masses.
MC/AP=AS One who handles people. Knowing and knowledge of people. To have many acquaintances.
AS/AP=MC To be a known personality in the near environment. The native as a customer. To be known in the scientific world.
#
MC/AS=AD To have firmly established relations to other people.
MC/AD=AS A person who is not able to be influenced. One who shows direct disrespect for the feelings of other people. To be without feeling for other people.
AS/AD=MC To feel closely connected to the location of the home. The sense of settling down. Others look up to the native and feel inferior. To stick to the given connections.
#
MC/AS=VU Joint proceedings. To exercise power with other people.
MC/VU=AS To let other people feel one's power and influence.
AS/VU=MC To be taxed, to be drawn upon heavily by others.
#
MC/AS=PO Intellectual agreement with others. A high ideal of one's quality of life.
MC/PO=AS To fuse one's ideas with those of others.
AS/PO=MC To exchange mentally and spiritually with others. Mental influence through others.
#
MC/SO=MO One's personal attitude toward women. One's own mind. To represent oneself to the public with heart and soul.
MC/MO=SO The manner in which the state of mind influences the body. The glandular system. The daily mental influence and its effect on the body.
SO/MO=MC The body, mind and soul in harmony. Feelings of friendship.
#
MC/SO=KN The native's relationships have, to the same extent, a physical and mental influence on him.
MC/KN=SO Soul unions affect the body.
SO/KN=MC Soul unions. To be involved in something body and soul.
#
MC/SO=ME Possessing agility of body and of soul. The condition of the nerves. One's own thinking. To contemplate.
MC/ME=SO Thoughts about one's own body. To philosophize. Physical agility.
SO/ME=MC The concept of the ego. The type of mind. The desire to appear young.
#
MC/SO=VE Harmony between body and soul. To make peace. Body and soul are devoted to love. To show one's preferences.
MC/VE=SO Self-love and vanity. Indulgent.
SO/VE=MC Perception of love. The love concept.
#
MC/SO=MA The heart and soul are put into the work. To proceed to action. To express the will. Making a decision. To make a complete job of it.
MC/MA=SO Administrator of justice. The jail warden. Offering resistance.
SO/MA=MC Masculinity. An aggressive body (Temperament). To be active. Interfering. To be industrious.
#
MC/SO=JU To be happy in both body and soul. Optimism. Contentment.
MC/JU=SO Physical well-being. Susceptible to flattery. Vanity.
SO/JU=MC Personal success and general well being. Lucky times. One's own feeling of happiness.
#
MC/SO=SA The body and soul are inhibited. Pessimist, Separation. To give up a particular objective. Sadness and grief. Takes life seriously. Out of work.
MC/SA=SO Personal opponents. To worry. To become feeble from sorrows. Physical separations.
SO/SA=MC To be forsaken. To retire. Rheumatic pains.
#
MC/SO=UR To experience strong physical and mental tension. Conflicts. Nervousness and sudden outbursts.
MC/UR=SO To become enraged. To be nervously excited. To be physically excited.
SO/UR=MC To experience a psychological shock. One who is easily excited. Frequent irritability. Sudden physically exciting events.
#
MC/SO=NE To be strongly sensitive in body and soul and to react to all influences, even the weak. Slackening, therefore debility and dislike. Dislike of everything.
MC/NE=SO Cosmetics. Sleight-of-hand. Personal weaknesses, secrets or want of clarity. Bodily deception. Felling guilty.
SO/NE=MC Confusion because of self-deception. To be receptive but also hypersensitive. One who is disinclined. Delicate.
#
MC/SO=PL To be and to remain adaptable and capable. Big changes in life. Alterations between the body and soul.
MC/PL=SO To concentrate on the condition and development of one's body.
SO/PL=MC One's personal taste. The attitude toward nourishment and physical development.
#
MC/SO=CU Physical and psychological well-being in married life. True also in company with others or in the arts.
MC/CU=SO To marry. To be a performer in the arts. Member of a community, association, corporation.
SO/CU=MC The native as a member of the family as an artist, or member of the community.
#
MC/SO=HA Defects of the body react upon the soul and the reverse. This results in physical discomfort and ill humor. To feel disgusted.
MC/HA=SO Ill humored on account of sickness. To grieve over the poor condition of one's body. Grief through male persons.
SO/HA=MC To be misshapen. To be sensible of bodily defects and restrictions. Not to be recognized or to be suppressed.
#
MC/SO=ZE Courageous, energetic and wanting to lead. To have to interfere. The creative drive of the body and soul.
MC/ZE=SO To spur the body on. Wanting to accomplish a great deal physically. To be a sportsman with body and soul.
SO/ZE=MC To be fit to be a leader. To display might. To be creatively active. To perceive a conflagration.
#
MC/SO=KR To be loyal to the authorities with heart and soul, A high personage.
MC/KR=SO To strive for independence. To exert personal authority.
SO/KR=MC Independence. Grandeur in one's demeanor. Highbirth. Of royal birth.
#
MC/SO=AP To aspire with body and soul. To be successful.
MC/AP=SO Success as a scientist or through life experience. The correct use of the senses. To have very extensive connections.
SO/AP=MC To be successful. To derive honors and dignities.
#
MC/SO=AD To have great perseverance. Holding tenaciously to ideas.
MC/AD=SO Insensible in respect to the body. To suppress pain. To create a resistant body.
SO/AD=MC Obstinate and steadfast, Wanting patiently. To retire into oneself. Great firmness or steadfastness of the body.
#
MC/SO=VU Union of power in body and soul.
MC/VU=SO Possessing great physical strength and vigor and to use it.
SO/VU=MC Feeling of power through one's own physical strength.
#
MC/SO=PO Union of a delicate body and a delicate, highly sensitive soul.
MC/PO=SO The mind of the native stipulates bodily form and appearance.
SO/PO=MC Mental and spiritual inclinations. To have a delicate body.
#
MC/MO=KN Relationships that influence the mind. Forming soul unions with women.
MC/KN=MO The emotions that are the result of soul unions.
MO/KN=MC Sympathy toward women.
#
MC/MO=ME Thoughts are stimulated by women. One's own active thoughts.
MC/ME=MO A sensitive mind. Thinking that is flexible and changeable. Thoughts about females. Opinions.
MO/ME=MC One's own mental flexibility, instability. To be impressed easily. Also to forget easily. To be superficial and always looking for the new. To be business like.
#
MC/MO=VE To have a preference for women. A harmonious mind. Feminine natured.
MC/VE=MO To have a preference for females.
MO/VE=MC To be adored by women. To have others mother oneself. To be a mother.
#
MC/MO=MA Sexual stimulation of women. One who is able to be firm. Mentally decisive and organized. The attitude of a soldier.
MC/MA=MO The correct use and division of one's time. To give orders to women.
MO/MA=MC The wakeful state of the brain. Consciousness. Day-consciousness. One pays attention, is wide awake. One's own wife. The metal contact with the wife. The personal attitude toward the time of work.
#
MC/MO=JU A good-natured person with a fortunate state of mind. The time of confinement (births). Happy hours for women.
MC/JU=MO To be lucky with women. Harmonious connections with women.
MO/JU=MC Contentment. Many happy hours in the life-time. Being made happy by women. To be pregnant.
#
MC/MO=SA The personal relations to the public are upsetting or disturbed. Experiencing grief through females (melancholy).
MC/SA=MO To avoid or to deny public appearance. To separate from others. The frame of mind due to the separation from a woman.
MO/SA=MC Mentally depressed woman. Man deserted by his wife. To be serious. To retire, to be in reserve. To keep distant, to be conservative.
#
MC/MO=UR Women in the state of excitement (labor-pains). Excitement in general. No sense of timing.
MC/UR=MO To be in a bad humor on account of a woman or the public. To enact a scene. Labor pains.
MO/UR=MC Attentive. Capable of quick action. To show interest ... wanting to take part. To be under steady mental tenseness. Mentally irritable.
#
MC/MO=NE To be annoyed by a woman. Women who are deceitful. The "straying fancy.".
MC/NE=MO To be very fantastic. Sleepwalker. To deceive females or the public. Sensitive to beams (radiated energy).
MO/NE=MC Sensibility. Touchiness. The sense of smell, state of dreaming. To live in a dream-world. Not facing the realities of life, not able to recognize them or to recognize them in due course. Mentally impressed through secrecies of female persons.
#
MC/MO=PL Changes in public that impress the mind. Changes that relate the native to the public in a personal manner. Tends to be infirm of purpose or unsteady.
MC/PL=MO One's influence on women or the public as regards their development.
MO/PL=MC To be exposed to hourly and faster changes. Continuous readjusting. To be "John� on the spot." Likes to attend to his affairs without delay.
#
MC/MO=CU Soul harmony in marriage. The community sense is directed by the mind. A community minded inclination.
MC/CU=MO To be a performer in the arts (female). In the company of women. Dealings with congregated women. Public community associations.
MO/CU=MC To be a fiancee. Fiancee of the native. Social inclination of native. Hours of leisure in the company of women. Favors. Large social clubs, balls, dancing.
#
MC/MO=HA To have trouble with a woman. Women who are vile-natured. A low mentality.
MC/HA=MO To get into a tight spot on account of a woman. To have worries over women. Grieving about conditions in the public. The grieving populace.
MO/HA=MC Calumniation through malicious women. To be enmeshed by bad women. Fiancee of low standards. Slandered, worried.
#
MC/MO=ZE To be stimulated by women into creative activity. A fiery mind and creative, active brain.
MC/ZE=MO To work in connection with women or the public. To be stimulated to work by a woman.
MO/ZE=MC Fiery disposition of the mind. Creative talent. Disciplined behavior. To utilize the time for creative work.
#
MC/MO=KR One who possesses good mental qualities and is generous.
MC/KR=MO To fill a public office. Deputies, general overseers and managers.
MO/KR=MC Nobility of soul. Mental influence by independent women. To belong to the leading people. Civil servants.
#
MC/MO=AP To be frank, candid and big-hearted.
MC/AP=MO Anthropology. Psychic experiences. Dealings with women and the public.
MO/AP=MC Greatness of soul. On a large scale, generous. To be in touch with several nations. To be impressed by them.
#
MC/MO=AD To be reserved and introverted. Inclined to narrow minded refusals or rejections.
MC/AD=MO To pull oneself together in order to remain steadfast. One who is disinclined toward tender feminine feelings.
MO/AD=MC Narrow minded. To live in crowded conditions. Mentally impressed by narrowness of space of the own people. The minute of death.
#
MC/MO=VU The power of the mind. One who is powerfully influenced by women. Experiencing influential public associations. One who is influenced by the public.
MC/VU=MO Executive powers. To strongly influence women and the public. To make a great stir.
MO/VU=MC The native is the cause of a great surprise to the public. To deal with people of public influence, and be impressed by them. There is a powerful mutual influence and interest between the public and the native.
#
MC/MO=PO A gifted medium. The spiritual-mental attitude.
MC/PO=MO To possess a remarkable perceptive faculty. In this way to influence women and the public.
MO/PO=MC Mentally prepared. Susceptible to spiritual things. To be influenced by spiritualized people.
#
MC/KN=ME Mental stimulation resulting from soul unions. Thought connections.
MC/ME=KN Thoughts concern the beginning of a new relationship.
KN/ME=MC One's own correspondence or exchange of thoughts. One's own train of thinking.
#
MC/KN=VE Personal love connections. Soulful love union.
MC/VE=KN To show affection for someone. Receptive to establishing relations.
KN/VE=MC To be in love. To be the object or affections. Harmonious connections.
#
MC/KN=MA An erotic soul union. To seek fellowship in one's work.
MC/MA=KN To have the power to command others.
KN/MA=MC Inclinations to flirtations. Mutual activities with others. Common working interests with others.
#
MC/KN=JU Happiness through one's own relationships. To share the feeling of bliss with another.
MC/JU=KN Harmonious ties and good luck through them.
KN/JU=MC General popularity. To be well liked.
#
MC/KN=SA Gloom cast by others. To lose a connection.
MC/SA=KN Burdening relationships. To be in mourning. To be united by sorrow. To say good-bye. To sever a relationship.
KN/SA=MC To be deserted by the husband. To incline to separation. To get notice of dismissal.
#
MC/KN=UR Sudden connections. To experience sudden emotion.
MC/UR=KN Connections that cause mental strain. Unusual relationships. Sudden bonds.
KN/UR=MC The desire for innovations. To be connected with new things.
#
MC/KN=NE Dissolution of connections. Unreliable associations.
MC/NE=KN To induce others to commit fraudulent acts. Errors and deception in relationships.
KN/NE=MC To see oneself disappointed and deceived in one's own connections. To feel deserted by all.
#
MC/KN=PL The influence of one's own development. The beginning of or change of associations.
MC/PL=KN To bring other persons under one's own influence. Relationships which cause a personal alteration in development.
KN/PL=MC Changes in the connections, meeting new people.
#
MC/KN=CU A psychic relationship with the family or the arts.
MC/CU=KN Seeking relationships with sociable people such as artists, groups, family or art associations.
KN/CU=MC To enter into a betrothal. To marry. The influence of familiar or social connections upon the native. Sense for society and art.
#
MC/KN=HA To suffer a strong mental depression. To be linked with evil matters.
MC/HA=KN Grief and sorrow with other people.
KN/HA=MC To be forced to deal with base and vulgar people. To have to stand for insults and abuse. To be connected professionally with criminals. A " fence.".
#
MC/KN=ZE To be incited. To be aroused mentally.
MC/ZE=KN Team-work. Wanting to be active with a number of other people.
KN/ZE=MC To be elected or selected. The driver. Wishing to produce something with several others. To force oneself or to submit to force.
#
MC/KN=KR Mentally elevated associations.
MC/KR=KN Moguls among themselves. Relationship with people who have abilities.
KN/KR=MC A leader among leading men. Connected with leading men. Professional intercourse with the authorities.
#
MC/KN=AP Your own choice of relationships. Extensive, far reaching associations. Ties with scientists, commerce or trades people.
MC/AP=KN To congregate with many people. Meeting scientific people.
KN/AP=MC To feel free. Mental and emotional experience with many. To be connected with many in science or business.
#
MC/KN=AD To be limited by connections. It is difficult to form ties with others. Inclined to sterile connections.
MC/AD=KN To find stable connections with others. One who seeks acquaintance with steadfast people.
KN/AD=MC To feel attracted by oppressed people. Connections with lonely types of people who are avoided or who are ill reputed. To retain only a few connections. Connections which are separated through death. To feel in bonds or to long for freedom.
#
MC/KN=VU To be fortunate to develop powerful and influential contacts.
MC/VU=KN To generate an impressive influence through public connections.
KN/VU=MC To be involved in violent measures, to suffer from them. Connected with great mechanical forces. To have mighty connections.
#
MC/KN=PO To have and to seek mental connections (spiritual soul-unions).
MC/PO=KN To form a mental partnership with other people.
KN/PO=MC The native has mental and spiritual connections. The mental connections affect the native.
#
MC/ME=VE To think about acquaintances. One's attitude about love and art.
MC/VE=ME To make acquaintances easily. To discuss beauty and enjoy beautiful objects.
ME/VE=MC Acquaintanceship. To have and make friends. Appreciation of beauty. Artistic sense.
#
MC/ME=MA To concentrate on work. Opinions concerning one's own work.
MC/MA=ME To give orders to others. Acting quickly. To act rashly.
ME/MA=MC The own way of talking. Quickly resolving. Love of fighting. To be in a state of excitement. Haste.
#
MC/ME=JU To be elated and cheerful. Thinking of luck and success. To have wishes. To laugh up one's sleeve.
MC/JU=ME To enjoy talking. Mental tranquility and contentment.
ME/JU=MC To be in a happy contented mood. Optimism.
#
MC/ME=SA Thinking about separation or travel. Grief, bereavement, distress.
MC/SA=ME To be sorrowful or brooding. To comprehend slowly. To subject oneself to thoughts of separation. To be obliged to separate.
ME/SA=MC Logic-philosophically inclined. Fond of travel. Unsettled. Inconstant.
#
MC/ME=UR To be suddenly notified. To have spontaneous thoughts. To become enraged or excited.
MC/UR=ME Tension created by a call. To become energetic. To use harsh words.
ME/UR=MC To have original thoughts. Technical or mathematical talent. Energetic thinking.
#
MC/ME=NE The mind is blank. Thinking of nothing. Not able to recognize important points. To be absentminded. To be prepared for something or to receive intuitive thoughts.
MC/NE=ME Intuitive, false, imagined or fraudulent thinking or feelings. Thoughts and the way of expressing them are not clear and distinct.
ME/NE=MC Gifted with intuition. To blend fiction and truth. To be inclined to untruthfulness.
#
MC/ME=PL To come to a conclusion. Logical thinking. To think of transformations and change. To come to conclusions.
MC/PL=ME The development of the cogitative faculty (meditative, to ponder deeply). The forming of ideas.
ME/PL=MC Thinking and understanding are one thing. The capability of adjusting one's thoughts. The mobility of the mind co-ordinates well with external nimbleness and adaptability.
#
MC/ME=CU To contemplate marriage. To meditate on art.
MC/CU=ME The amateur. Artistic and social thinking. Lover of the arts.
ME/CU=MC To be favored by the 'Muse." Artistically gifted. Socially inclined.
#
MC/ME=HA To prepare an atrocity. To work out the details. Contemplation of evil.
MC/HA=ME Ponderous thoughts. Stupidity. To keep bad things to oneself. To meditate about miserable things. Wishing evil on others.
ME/HA=MC To harbor bad thoughts, to hide them.
#
MC/ME=ZE To exercise self-restraint. Self control.
MC/ZE=ME To have creative thoughts. Wanting always to start something new. To make proposals.
ME/ZE=MC To outdo oneself. To obey a command.
#
MC/ME=KR To make observations about the power of the state ... the President or authorities in general. Thoughts are directed toward the higher things of life. To be self-complacent.
MC/KR=ME To express a great and high trend of thoughts. To make arrangements. When he/she speaks, people listen to what is said. To speak with authority.
ME/KR=MC Good mental ability.
#
MC/ME=AP To speak several languages. Talkative. Thinking about trade and traffic. Scientific thinking.
MC/AP=ME Knowledge of languages. Scientific talks.
ME/AP=MC Letting the thoughts wander far. Much thinking. Free and open minded. To be capable of scientific thinking.
#
MC/ME=AD To be a blockhead. Experiencing grave hindrances due to the thinking. To keep serious thoughts. Melancholy.
MC/AD=ME One who finds it hard to talk ... slow thinking.
ME/AD=MC To think about a matter thoroughly. To be serious or sad, to grieve, to feel depressed by an unpleasant condition. Pessimist.
#
MC/ME=VU One's own power of thought. Good comprehension. A quick mind. An abundance of ideas.
MC/VU=ME A powerful thought process. Flexible and mobile thinking.
ME/VU=MC Strong ability of thinking, ability to think fast.
#
MC/ME=PO One who has many ideas and whose thoughts are on the spiritual plane.
MC/PO=ME idealistic way of thinking. One's own individual ideas.
ME/PO=MC To appear spiritualized. To be the carrier of an idea. To become familiar with ideas.
#
MC/VE=MA Sexual attraction and desire.
MC/MA=VE To be sexually stimulated. To stimulate others.
VE/MA=MC To inflame. Desire. Intimate acquaintances. Personal relation or attitude towards blood relationship. To work with love and devotion.
#
MC/VE=JU The sensation of happiness and love. Wishes that come true.
MC/JU=VE To enjoy a blissful feeling because of love.
VE/JU=MC The sensation of love. To be recipient of love's joy.
#
MC/VE=SA Sorrow resulting from separation in love. Hindrances and sorrow in love. To long for a separation.
MC/SA=VE Love's sorrow. To separate from love, women, or very agreeable things.
VE/SA=MC Flirtations. Hindrances and obstacles in love. Illicit intercourse. Illegitimate birth. He or she being forsaken.
#
MC/VE=UR Amateur. A favored pursuit. Sudden affection. To be inflamed with affection.
MC/UR=VE To become daring. Tension through love, pleasure or diversion. Longing for love.
VE/UR=MC One's own refined feeling of love. To be suddenly in love.
#
MC/VE=NE To renounce love. Lover's grief. Hopeless love relationships. Love without prospects.
MC/NE=VE Ideas on love and affection are not clear and distinct. Wrong perception of love. To pretend love.
VE/NE=MC Hopes are not realized. False love or sex instinct. Love's sorrow.
#
MC/VE=PL One who loves change. To long for change and look for a transformation.
MC/PL=VE To develop harmoniously.
VE/PL=MC The own harmonious development. Development of own wishes and hopes.
#
MC/VE=CU Betrothal, social life, inclination to marriage. Artistic.
MC/CU=VE To have enthusiasm. The love of harmonious company. An eye for beauty.
VE/CU=MC To be endowed with much love in married life. To be in harmonious company or society. The love and affection to the native from community.
#
MC/VE=HA Attraction or preference for base love or immorality.
MC/HA=VE Grief because of love. Vulgar lust and feelings. To be pleased with vulgar love.
VE/HA=MC Serving, submissive soul. To grieve. Servant. Prostitute or souteneur (procurer, pimp).
#
MC/VE=ZE To wish to be desired. To wish for progeny. The sower.
MC/ZE=VE Wishing to produce something beautiful. Producing with love.
VE/ZE=MC To be devoted to one's work. The progenitor (direct ancestor or originator of a line of descent). The carrier of seed, the sower.
#
MC/VE=KR Love of father, superiors or people of importance. To have an idealistic point of view concerning love.
MC/KR=VE Charitable inclinations. To distribute gifts. To make gifts.
VE/KR=MC Take part in charitable activities. Charitably disposed. Receive benefits.
#
MC/VE=AP Peaceful inclinations. To seek friendship. To be inclined toward art and science.
MC/AP=VE Personal advantages through the many friends acquired in life.
VE/AP=MC Peaceful thinking. To be faithful and reliable. The own disposition and inclination toward faithfulness. Affection to the native is shown by many.
#
MC/VE=AD Longing to be calm, quiet and alone. One whose love does not find a response.
MC/AD=VE To be steadfast in one's sympathies.
VE/AD=MC Reserved in demonstrating one's feelings. Connected with few in faithful love and friendship. To like to meditate.
#
MC/VE=VU To personally desire something. One's own love increases to passion.
MC/VU=VE A tremendous passion in love.
VE/VU=MC A passionate disposition. To be compelled and motivated by an inner force.
#
MC/VE=PO To revere and idolize something. Strong religious inclinations. Longing for religion. Love which turns away from the worldly.
MC/PO=VE inclination, disposition and attitude toward religion.
VE/PO=MC To be faithful. One's own religious inclination.
#
MC/MA=JU To deal honestly. Intending to become engaged. Successful action is taken. The desire to make oneself happy.
MC/JU=MA Betrothal. Good luck from one's own activity. To be delighted over an act of love.
MA/JU=MC The betrothed person. Satisfaction and contentment in one's activity. Content with oneself.
#
MC/MA=SA Not wanting to become involved. To turn away or refrain from. Orders bringing about losses. Retarding actions are taken. To wait.
MC/SA=MA To terminate or interrupt work. Sorrows brought on by one's own actions. To be in mourning.
MA/SA=MC Grief. To suffer from separations or separating actions. Decease. Cause of death. Dying. Deaths of others that make a deep impression upon the mind of the native. One's own death.
#
MC/MA=UR Impulsive action. The possibility of becoming violent. Expressing oneself in an explosive manner. Exciting consequences of actions taken.
MC/UR=MA A spontaneous decision. To beat about oneself. Sudden physical attacks on others.
MA/UR=MC To get excited while working. Dare-devil. To be very violent. To suffer violence from others. Personal acts of violence.
#
MC/MA=NE A fraudulent action. Inability to assert oneself. To succumb to the situation. A weak will. To destroy something.
MC/NE=MA Swindler. Arrant knave. Not sure of one's attitude. Irresolute in decision. Work which is unsatisfactory and is never finished. Occupation with the unknown.
MA/NE=MC The native himself in a rejecting mood. Hopes which prove false. To experience failures and Losses. Sorrow on account of disease.
#
MC/MA=PL Performing several jobs at the same time. To build something up. An organizer. To have more than one iron in the fire.
MC/PL=MA The development of the will. Splitting one's force. Growth through action.
MA/PL=MC Organizer. Maker of plans. Attending to several jobs at the same time, To do them alternately.
#
MC/MA=CU A decision to marry. To be ready to act.
MC/CU=MA Intending to marry. Working together with others. Belonging to a military union. Works of art.
MA/CU=MC Intention to marry. Activity in a community.
#
MC/MA=HA To do things in a nasty way. The leader of a gang of criminals. A murderer. To be disconsolate. Taking revenge.
MC/HA=MA Sorrow or damage through one's own actions. Hesitate to deal ... lazy. Little initiative. Wanting to commit evil acts.
MA/HA=MC To suffer through mean acts of others. Quickly annoyed and weary. To be fed up and to have one's fill. To be separated mentally through the mean actions of others. To want to revenge oneself. An eye for an eye. To be disposed to atrocities or murder. Ugly deeds which bounce back.
#
MC/MA=ZE To make arrangements that have to be carried out immediately. To force a matter through. No opposition tolerated.
MC/ZE=MA Always ready to help. Daredevil, leader, good strategist. To force something against one's will.
MA/ZE=MC Creative, fiery soul.
#
MC/MA=KR A government executive. Policemen. Honorable and dignified dealings.
MC/KR=MA Acting independently and strictly on his/her own. Foreman, police captain, officer, sportsman, boxer, wrestler.
MA/KR=MC The ability to act independently. Craving for power. To wish to assert oneself.
#
MC/MA=AP Arrangements on a grand scale. To be prudent. Science or commerce.
MC/AP=MA Elaborate activities in commerce, industry and science.
MA/AP=MC Bold, open minded, cheerful. To unfold large, peaceful scientific or trade activity. Generous character. Ready to act. Comprehensive activity.
#
MC/MA=AD Condemned to inactivity. A limited sphere of work. Actions meet with grave resistance. To busy oneself with broken pieces, ruins, wreckage.
MC/AD=MA Very persevering in work or activity.
MA/AD=MC Narrow minded, mean or petty. To confine oneself to a small circle. To experience suppression. Limitation of freedom or movement. Activity in a small circle.
#
MC/MA=VU A mighty willpower. Energetic.
MC/VU=MA An intense urge for action.
MA/VU=MC Great efforts. To expect a fight. To express courage. To take and give violence. To develop great energies. Qualified for powerful deeds.
#
MC/MA=PO One's moral mode of dealing. Mentally active.
MC/PO=MA Mental independence. To differ mentally from others.
MA/PO=MC The personal and moral behavior. Exposed to moral influences. To be an ethical or moral example. Mental activity.
#
MC/JU=SA To be glad over a separation. Parting without sorrow.
MC/SA=JU A change of residence. To lose of burden. To "crowd one's luck".
JU/SA=MC Worries through losses.
#
MC/JU=UR Sudden happiness and luck.
MC/UR=JU To be enjoyed by others. Suddenly gladdened by a happy surprise. Luck.
JU/UR=MC Pleasure over a stroke of luck or good fortune.
#
MC/JU=NE A premonition of loss. Disappointment with expected luck.
MC/NE=JU A money swindler. A speculator. Imaginary luck. To imagine or pretend luck.
JU/NE=MC To be unburdened. Not to bother about anything. Spendthrift ... speculator.
#
MC/JU=PL Joy because of a lucky transformation. To be delighted over growing happiness because of the way things are developing.
MC/PL=JU Fortunate development of soul and spirit.
JU/PL=MC To be fortunate in one's own development. To experience pleasant transformations.
#
MC/JU=CU A pleasant feeling in society. Contented with married life. Luck through art.
MC/CU=JU Contentment and happiness through occupations dealing with art, marriage or community.
JU/CU=MC One' s own fortunate marriage or partnership. Publicly appearing artist. One's own happy family life.
#
MC/JU=HA Contentment in modest circumstances.
MC/HA=JU To be sad on account of money or other losses. Clouded happiness.
JU/HA=MC Shrinkage and decrease of fortune. Worried by restrictions or lack of money.
#
MC/JU=ZE Happiness over accomplishments.
MC/ZE=JU To urge others to laugh. To be humorous or hilarious. To promote pleasure.
JU/ZE=MC Delight in creating.
#
MC/JU=KR Fortunate as a leader. To be conscious of one's own authority. Self-conscious.
MC/KR=JU Success through one's own initiative and independence. Moneyed man, financier. Money or property increase.
JU/KR=MC To feel happy. To consider oneself as the Chief.
#
MC/JU=AP Happy expansion. Luck or money through science or commerce.
MC/AP=JU Happy experiences. Luck and success in commerce and science.
JU/AP=MC To be lucky. To handle money. To be fortunate in commerce or science.
#
MC/JU=AD Luck through treasures of the soil or matters concerning the soil. A little luck. Fortunate while at rest. In some instances can indicate the end of good fortune.
MC/AD=JU To be fortunate in mineral resources or soil conservation. To be insensible or indifferent towards good luck.
JU/AD=MC Not to be upset, composure in critical moments. Fortified with equanimity, that nothing can throw him off. The native retains his poise under the most trying circumstances. To bide one's time.
#
MC/JU=VU To have great optimism.
MC/VU=JU The optimist. Good luck and success through the exercise of personal power.
JU/VU=MC To be an optimist. To trust to others, and to be trusted. To have good luck.
#
MC/JU=PO Good luck through expressing a sensible attitude. Spiritually happy.
MC/PO=JU Lucky mental disposition. To search for spiritual contentment. Longing for wisdom.
JU/PO=MC To have wisdom and intelligence. To listen to the voice of conscience and reason. The ability to judge what is right and wrong (one's sense of judgement).
#
MC/SA=UR Sorrow and excitement caused by opponents and hindrances. To be the cause of sorrows and separations. To experience sudden sadness. To cause sudden separations.
MC/UR=SA To rid oneself of something. Throwing things out. To be quarrelsome. Irritation through losses, separation and opposing resistance.
SA/UR=MC To suffer from quarrels. Dealing with quarrelsome people. To be separated suddenly.
#
MC/SA=NE To grieve over the falseness of others. Sorrow through want of clearness or through resistance.
MC/NE=SA To cause losses or weakness or neglect. To suffer or separate through having a wrong concept (notion) of the situation.
SA/NE=MC To be diseases, to feel sick. To be under the spell or impression of a long and unpleasant chronic condition.
#
MC/SA=PL To feel downcast because of an interruption of one's own development. Slow development. To be sad over a divorce. Inclines to hesitate. The aggravating characteristic of frequently being late for appointments.
MC/PL=SA One's unfoldment suffers hindrances, obstacles - slows up. To be successful in causing a deliberate delay. Delays caused be the native.
SA/PL=MC To develop slowly. Hard to adapt oneself. To separate oneself easily from something. To be inclined for divorce.
#
MC/SA=CU Family in mourning. To separate from the family. To be oppressed because of separation.
MC/CU=SA A liking for funeral music. The state of mind after divorce. The "sense of community" experiences setbacks. To be a serious companion.
SA/CU=MC Joy or sorrow through separation in married life or of associates or partners.
#
MC/SA=HA To cause enemies because of one's own character. To be out of work. Grief through sickness or other causes.
MC/HA=SA Homeless. The stranger. Sorrow through one's own actions. Grief through sickness and separation. To be unemployed.
SA/HA=MC Melancholy disposition. To be depressed through terrible events. To resign. To become seriously ill. Great poverty. To suffer losses through diseases and hostility.
#
MC/SA=ZE Cares of fatherhood. Compelled to quit one's aims. Limited activity. Worries over support.
MC/ZE=SA Activities combined with hindrances, sorrows, money losses. Losses from inventions. Thefts to patents (infringements).
SA/ZE=MC Incendiary. Separation and losses through activity.
#
MC/SA=KR Tax worries. A sense of responsibility towards the family.
MC/KR=SA Decrease and losses through one's own initiative. An owner of an estate. Dealing in agricultural produce. An authority on the soil.
SA/KR=MC To be obstructed in independence.
#
MC/SA=AP To be a teacher or instructor. Necessary and frequent separations from groups of people. To separate from many people.
MC/AP=SA To be a pupil. To be fortunate through following the correct methods and in the area of education.
SA/AP=MC To have a methodical disposition. To proceed systematically. Pedantic. To experience many separations.
#
MC/SA=AD To be crowded and hindered for a long time. To be in a dilemma. "I can and must wait." To have great patience.
MC/AD=SA Enduring a dilemma. To be settled.
SA/AD=MC To be hindered. To be tied to something. To be patient. The ability to wait. To be disinterested. To cross a valley. To be in a tight spot. (a bottleneck).
#
MC/SA=VU To act in a guarded manner. To have strong inhibitions. To interfere by hindering the situation.
MC/VU=SA inclined to hinder. To guard or control something.
SA/VU=MC To be vigilant. Intense guarding. To watch very closely. To have an eye to one's own interests. Not easily deceived (it may be that these characteristic qualities do not appear until the native has some bad experiences).
#
MC/SA=PO To be an educator. To cause hindrances on the mental plane.
MC/PO=SA To be a student. To be brought up and educated.
SA/PO=MC One's own behavior. The pupil or the educator. To be an example. To separate or differentiate mentally.
#
MC/UR=NE To excite oneself in vain. Much noise about nothing or over something unintelligible.
MC/NE=UR Unconscious matters related to the beyond. Unconsciousness. To be the cause of sudden events. Aggravations caused by deceit, falsehood or dishonesty. To put into chaos and disorder.
UR/NE=MC I am unconscious. I am put out. I cannot think and act clearly. Anesthesia. To be in mourning. Communication with the deceased.
#
MC/UR=PL To be pushed back and forth by one's impulses. Sudden development and changes. To adapt or adjust oneself to something by leaps and bounds.
MC/PL=UR One's development is uneven, it leaps. To experience sudden changes of the mind, turn-abouts.
UR/PL=MC To be adaptable. The talent of applying oneself to changed conditions. To change by leaps and bounds. To be exposed to jerking changes.
#
MC/UR=CU Marrying quickly. Wedding joys. Stimulation, excitement and social tension.
MC/CU=UR A sudden marriage. To be original and enterprising in society. To have a taste for rhythmic art.
UR/CU=MC To get married or betrothed suddenly.
#
MC/UR=HA To execute plans for murder. Excitement through ugly notions. Misdeeds.
MC/HA=UR To be saddened and distressed through sudden death and adverse conditions.
UR/HA=MC To suffer from mean and vile acts. To suddenly experience loathsomeness and disgust. To be the cause of death. In danger of being murdered.
#
MC/UR=ZE To be surprised by one's own decisions. To have and pursue a direction or goal unerringly. To reach one's goal. Decidedly self-willed. To enjoy fires. To set a fire.
MC/ZE=UR Suddenly wanting to be creatively active. Sudden urge for great deeds. To force one's will on someone.
UR/ZE=MC Energetic willpower. The dictator. Subjected to force.
#
MC/UR=KR Tension caused by a sudden test of strength. To issue a sudden order.
MC/KR=UR Wanting to do something of importance on a grand scale. One who suddenly demonstrates leadership skills which causes excitement and surprise. Manager of a technical establishment.
UR/KR=MC To be tense and loaded with energy and strength. Tyrant. Despot in business. Dictator. To be subject to someone's power.
#
MC/UR=AP Sudden expansion. Sudden unexpected success. Astrological success.
MC/AP=UR The astrologer. Ties in the science of technology. Sudden experiences.
UR/AP=MC To cause tenseness of many. To be under tension with many. To look for coming experiences with great interest and tenseness. Interest in new things. To suddenly gain experiences. To be an astrologer.
#
MC/UR=AD Very difficult to appease. To tear up, to break up, to interrupt something suddenly. To scatter shreds around.
MC/AD=UR To be frightened or suddenly upset. Delicacy.
UR/AD=MC To experience murder or other violent commotions. To be in danger of being murdered.
#
MC/UR=VU Irritable character. Suddenly using force.
MC/VU=UR The sudden exercise of authority and power creating great excitement.
UR/VU=MC To express oneself in an explosive way, to concentrate all power on one point. To be exposed to suddenly breaking out forces of nature. A bellicose character. Sudden mighty display of power.
#
MC/UR=PO To look through to the bottom of something. To make something lucid. To know the truth. To promote ideas and to generate propaganda.
MC/PO=UR To inform others. Mental clarity. Sudden realization of the truth. "I begin to find mi way.".
UR/PO=MC To be mentally alive and alert. Mental preparedness and tenseness. To strive for light, knowledge and truth.
#
MC/NE=PL To be cunning. Swindles which are not uncovered.
MC/PL=NE The evolution and transformation are hardly noticed on the surface. Creeping alterations.
NE/PL=MC To be misterious. To develop the future from the past. To be in the state of development or transformation where the consequences for the future cannot be visualized. To stay hidden, to keep in the background. To be ,missing.
#
MC/NE=CU To join others in following the unknown. False pretences. Marriage swindler. Forger in the arts.
MC/CU=NE A magician. To experience deception or confusion in social interaction. To be party to dissolving an organization. To remain a bungler in the arts. To quit art.
NE/CU=MC Grief through marriage. To be part of dissolution of a union.
#
MC/NE=HA To drown. To feel disgusted with deception. To sustain losses through lies, a lack of clarity or deception. To lead an evil life.
MC/HA=NE Grief because of falsehood, deceit or want of firmness. "I am drowning." To give up the last hope.
NE/HA=MC To commit oneself to great danger. Subjected to grave deceptions. To drown. Seafarer. Suffocation.
#
MC/NE=ZE To gravely offend someone. Enterprises that disappoint.
MC/ZE=NE Weakness of will. Incompetence. Planning too many things. Abandoning plans.
NE/ZE=MC Hot tempered. Pompous person.
#
MC/NE=KR To carry something through independently which is dubious, unknown or uncertain. Chief instigator of frauds. Deception of higher-ups.
MC/KR=NE To be unrecognized, rejected, overlooked. Failure, in particular cases, as a manager. Aeronautical captain ... Captain in general.
NE/KR=MC Incompetent as manager or leader.
#
MC/NE=AP Extensive deceptions and frauds.
MC/AP=NE Engaged in sciences of the future. To penetrate unknown fields of science. To work in a new field of science. To have peculiar experiences.
NE/AP=MC To embezzle. To be under a grand illusion or great deception. To be uncertain with many about the future. To look or go into the future with fear. To scientifically reveal the future.
#
MC/NE=AD To be repulsive. To radiate a cold nature. Situations that are injurious to one's own tactful nature. To experience changes from the bottom up. The changing environment.
MC/AD=NE One who is tactful and difficult to deceive. A talent for clearing the slate in order to start over. Able to wipe something out.
NE/AD=MC To change the profession. To enter into a new form of being. To come into different circumstances. To change into a different condition.
#
MC/NE=VU Pronounced refinement of feeling. Enormous imagination. To deceive or be deceived on a grand scale. To pretend to great power.
MC/VU=NE The dissolution of one's own power. To miss completely.
NE/VU=MC Easy to deceive or defraud. To experience a great disappointment. To have a mighty disappointed.
#
MC/NE=PO To simulate brains. The bluffer.
MC/PO=NE Mental delicacy. Enthusiastic. Not materialistically inclined.
NE/PO=MC To look into the mirror, to regard the reflected image, knowledge of oneself. Introspection. To be under the influence and the impression of the wrong faith and the wrong view of life. To be considered a follower of the wrong faith which is pernicious and highly destructive.
#
MC/PL=CU Hereditary transmission. Concerning ancestors and children (lineage). To "pick up the thread." Continuing the old trends. The development of a social consciousness, community sense or artistic talent.
MC/CU=PL The family tradition. Social changes take place. The development for and understanding of an artistic sense.
PL/CU=MC Taking part in the transformation of a community. To become a member of another club. Change in the social surroundings.
#
MC/PL=HA To be on the decline, downfall. To grow worse (backslide).
MC/HA=PL To grieve over one's decline. Self-denial. Sad transformations.
PL/HA=MC Personal failure to act. To be unreliable or dishonest. Never to get on in the world. To experience a decline. Decadence.
#
MC/PL=ZE The inner vocation. To develop and work up the performance. Development of leadership.
MC/ZE=PL To thrust oneself forward. Increased urge to accomplish something. To bring on a transformation.
PL/ZE=MC One's personal impulses. Sense of duty. To be conscientious. Eagerness to produce. To take over a duty.
#
MC/PL=KR From pupil to master, high standards. The tendency is up. To develop one's knowledge.
MC/KR=PL The desire to dominate. To be or to become a master.
PL/KR=MC To develop and show one's ability. Subjected to great tests in destiny as well as in daily life. To experience a great transformation.
#
MC/PL=AP Free spiritual or mental development.
MC/AP=PL One who makes a correct evaluation of his/her own experiences. Developing scientific abilities. Having to make many alterations in life.
PL/AP=MC The personal ability to develop. To collect experiences. To move In a new direction of development. To start large changes.
#
MC/PL=AD Development of persistence and firmness. Standstill of development. The debacle of a revolution. (During directions this axis indicates a deep reaching transformation).
MC/AD=PL To keep going in the same direction of development. To remain on course.
PL/AD=MC Able to change. A total transition that remains on course. A firm and complete metamorphosis in one's intellectual capacity to comprehend.
#
MC/PL=VU The development of one's might and power.
MC/VU=PL To be the cause of powerful changes and transformations.
PL/VU=MC To be under the impression and influence of a great change. Either our mental attitude is changed through outside events, or a change of our mental attitude makes us see the outside world differently.
#
MC/PL=PO The turning from materialism to spiritualism.
MC/PO=PL One's personal mental and spiritual development.
PL/PO=MC The ability to mentally change and develop. To fit oneself mentally into a different environment. If the native is mentally or spiritually in a higher state of development, this configuration takes him higher and higher. In a state of low development it brings inconsistency in his belief. Fickleness.
#
MC/CU=HA An artistic eye for antiques, ancient items of value, and objects of art. To collect and hoard valuable antique and art items. Also to hoard provisions and other things. A liking for vulgar art and company. To seek them out. To enjoy vulgar songs.
MC/HA=CU Grief through married life, organizations or associations. "I am worried about the future.".
CU/HA=MC Egoist. Individualist. To be part of a tragedy.
#
MC/CU=ZE A desire to produce something artistic with others.
MC/ZE=CU The native works with people around him. To bring something new in art. Creative artistic work.
CU/ZE=MC Founder of a corporation. To create artistically. To be a member of a military organization.
#
MC/CU=KR The chairman. A genius in the arts. A great talent for art. An association of independent persons.
MC/KR=CU Socially leading others. A great artist. Artistic genius.
CU/KR=MC Marriages. Great family celebration. To be present at family gatherings. One's own social position. Great artistic talent.
#
MC/CU=AP To have many members in the family. An active social life (entertainment). To be happy in company with many others. Common interests with scientists and representatives of commerce and trade.
MC/AP=CU An experienced art connoisseur. A friend of peace. Many connections develop in the field of art.
CU/AP=MC To belong to a great family or association. To have success as an artist or scientist.
#
MC/CU=AD To be avoided. One's wishes in family or community are denied.
MC/AD=CU Wanting to be alone. To reject or refuse to be with others.
CU/AD=MC The native has few but close contacts. To be lonely, to become isolated in a community or partnership. To be part in the dissolution of a partnership or community.
#
MC/CU=VU To have a great sense for community. To experience a mighty influence through social connections.
MC/VU=CU Might and influence through family and/or community.
CU/VU=MC Great talent for organizing. To be an outstanding artist. Mighty art creation.
#
MC/CU=PO Spiritual or religious communities. To be attracted to religious sects. To seek them out.
MC/PO=CU Mental attitudes toward marriage, community and art. To understand them from a religious point of view.
CU/PO=MC To seek mental or spiritual community. To be attracted by religious communions.
#
MC/HA=ZE To create disharmony. To be forced to start some evil. The urge to act on evil deeds.
MC/ZE=HA To wish to help the sick. Sorrow and hindrances at work. To be obliged to fight strong resistance. To work with coals, soot or dirt. To produce lowbrow art. To make up vulgar stories.
HA/ZE=MC To refuse, to be negative. Inclined to idleness. To be hated, or to hate. Exposed to mean tricks. To be paralyzed, compelled to be inactive. Incendiary, arson. Personal connection with industry.
#
MC/HA=KR To get into an ill humor because of interference of authority figures or the government. Damage caused by advancing independently.
MC/KR=HA To be one's own cause of sorrows and frustrations. To obtain something from the earth. Owner of a mine. Owner of a blast furnace (to work with heat and fire energies such as a potter with a kiln).
HA/KR=MC To experience great meanness, to suffer from it or to commit it. Loss of business or profession as a result of mismanagement.
#
MC/HA=AP Bad times for commerce and trade. To busy oneself with science of antiquity, secret science or history.
MC/AP=HA To have knowledge of antiquity. There is the danger of becoming involved in evil experiences.
HA/AP=MC To come through bad experience to knowledge. To be under the influence and impression of great poverty. To know the past. To be a historian. Knowledge of a secret doctrine.
#
MC/HA=AD Want for the simplest necessities of life. Sadness through privation, cold and need.
MC/AD=HA To be and to remain poor. However, indifferent to poverty or misfortune.
HA/AD=MC Coldness - hard to approach. To stay alone and lonely all through life. To live in scant circumstances.
#
MC/HA=VU Sorrow through dependence and poverty.
MC/VU=HA Encircled by danger. Ignoring danger. To deliberately commit oneself to dangerous situations. A fight against that which is repulsive.
HA/VU=MC Feeling of dependence. To be dependent on natural forces.
#
MC/HA=PO To possess secret knowledge. The negative influence indicates mental defects. The native appears to be an odd person in the mental respect.
MC/PO=HA To be living in the past. One's mental baseness and stupidity.
HA/PO=MC To be poor mentally or spiritually, to show defects. To be mentally an odd fellow. To represent a secret science, or wisdom.
#
MC/ZE=KR A great inventor. To work at something independently. To create something great. Manager of a large technical establishment.
MC/KR=ZE A foreman or manager of a machine works. Army officers. To lead without falling.
ZE/KR=MC To be energetically active. The will to see it through. Great strategist. War scientist.
#
MC/ZE=AP To be a scientific researcher.
MC/AP=ZE An interest in the natural sciences. Experiences in the field of engineering, technology and/or pyrotechnics.
ZE/AP=MC Gifted hands and nimble fingers. To be practical. To solve problems in a practical manner rather than by making a study of them.
#
MC/ZE=AD To overcome the difficulties of the beginning. Battles against strongest opposition.
MC/AD=ZE An aversion to starting something new, but after beginning keeps going.
ZE/AD=MC Ready to start, willingness to begin, ability to proceed. To give something shape, image and expression. To feel a new task as a burden.
#
MC/ZE=VU The urge for action cannot be stopped. The word "impossible" does not exist.
MC/VU=ZE Controlling abundant energies. To supervise machines.
ZE/VU=MC Mighty urge for activities. The strong-willed man who accomplishes things like a machine.
#
MC/ZE=PO Mental work or creations. To be a witness. To bear witness.
MC/PO=ZE To enforce mentally. To demonstrate something. To affirm (testify) to the truth.
ZE/PO=MC To firmly stand by one's conviction, To lead mentally. To take over the mental leadership. To be under a mental compulsion. To know ways and means of getting out. To convince and to be able to prove.
#
MC/KR=AP To be an authority in science, commerce or trade.
MC/AP=KR To pursue the political sciences. Becoming an authority for many people. One who enjoys an intellectually high standing.
KR/AP=MC Great knowledge and ability or to have or to gain great experience. To be an authority for many.
#
MC/KR=AD One's own capability finds no recognition. An authority in a small circle. The end of authority.
MC/AD=KR Perseverance as an authority.
KR/AD=MC To be noted for a certain peculiarity. To be different. To be an odd character and noted even among many.
#
MC/KR=VU To have the power of attorney. A mighty authority. To exercise the power of the state. Executive powers.
MC/VU=KR To have mighty authoritative influence. To wield the power of the state.
KR/VU=MC To possess great esteem and great influence. To wield great power. To give dictatorial orders. To exercise the power of the state.
#
MC/KR=PO Higher education. Intellectual leader. Spiritus rector.
MC/PO=KR A person of refinement and education. To be on a highly elevated intellectual plane. A spiritual leader.
KR/PO=MC High minded attitude, To have a revelation. To strive for wisdom and understanding. Averse to all material things.
#
MC/AP=AD To be content with personal achievements and saturated with satisfaction. One who has no more aspirations.
MC/AD=AP Self-sufficient. Forced to accept restrictions.
AP/AD=MC The contented man. To have peace of mind. To be mentally balanced. To feel satisfied. Lack of ambition.
#
MC/AP=VU Expanding the mental horizons. Major successes in one's personal experiences.
MC/VU=AP The successful wielding of power. Expansion.
AP/VU=MC To be personally successful. To have great influence. To exercise power.
#
MC/AP=PO One's own convictions. To be disinclined toward material things. To study spiritual sciences. To follow the mental or spiritual spheres in life.
MC/PO=AP A free-thinker. Breadth of spirit. Spiritually unfettered. One's own concept of the world.
AP/PO=MC The personality of the native as an expression of his disposition (way of thinking). To have, with many, the same mental attitude and emotional attitude.
#
MC/AD=VU To have great perseverance and powers of resistance.
MC/VU=AD Restrained. To suffer failures. To be suppressed or subjugated.
AD/VU=MC Great perseverance. To stay firm without wilting or bending. Hard in taking it. To be harshly treated by fate.
#
MC/AD=PO One who has a profound mind. To be educated and trained.
MC/PO=AD To be educated. Profundity of thought. To go back to the original cause.
AD/PO=MC One's own culture and education. To be cultured, educated, trained. To educate or be educated.
#
MC/VU=PO To have spiritual or mental influence or power.
MC/PO=VU To have a tremendous intellect.
VU/PO=MC To be enlightened by a great mind or spirit. Self assurance, Pride. Sense of honor, dignity. To possess a remarkable mental influence on others.
#
AS/SO=MO Social intercourse and physical relations with women.
AS/MO=SO Personal acquaintance with women. Easily made acquaintances. Many changes in acquaintances. Female relatives of the native.
SO/MO=AS The daily and hourly associations with other people. Friendship ties.
#
AS/SO=KN Connection through social intercourse.
AS/KN=SO Close personal ties. To physically interact with other people.
SO/KN=AS Personal relationships in the environment.
#
AS/SO=ME Relationships with similar thinking or equal types of people. Fellow students, schoolmates or teachers. A teaching or learning atmosphere. Mental. Relations to young students.
AS/ME=SO Exchange of thoughts about the native.
SO/ME=AS To be conscious of one's surroundings. Correspondence. Conversations. Aquaintances.
#
AS/SO=VE Love relationships. Harmony in associations with women. Pleasant social relations.
AS/VE=SO Love of others bestowed upon the body (To physically enjoy the love of others). Harmony in personal connections.
SO/VE=AS To be loved by others.
#
AS/SO=MA Co-workers. Social relations with male persons, soldiers and co-workers. Fights and brawls. To quarrel. Persons in the environment who are not master of their emotions and are not under control in this regard.
AS/MA=SO To be a co-worker or to have them. To get a thrashing. To be beaten up.
SO/MA=AS Other people act or interfere. To be physically in contact with another person. To act physically toward another person.
#
AS/SO=JU To enjoy advantages and support from relationships. To form associations that are very fortunate for the native.
