AS/JU=SO Pleasant associations.
SO/JU=AS To feel most comfortable when in the company of others. Extroverted and social disposition. One who is fortunate with other people.
#
AS/SO=SA Hindrances through others. Separations from acquaintances. Funerals.
AS/SA=SO To shake hands. Physical farewells. Alienations.
SO/SA=AS Personal difficulties through others or in general. Separations.
#
AS/SO=UR Suddenly meeting someone. Excitement and tension through other people. Sudden events caused by others.
AS/UR=SO Exciting events with others. To be menaced by others.
SO/UR=AS People who arouse strong feeling, stir up activity or stimulate the environment. A person who increases the energies of those around him/her through provoking the situation.
#
AS/SO=NE Physical weakness as compared to other people. To be deceived by persons closely connected or related. Damaging activity or slander. Secret activity in associations.
AS/NE=SO Personal or bodily losses through other persons, or other influences. Danger of poisoning.
SO/NE=AS An acquaintance with infirm people. Relations with sensitive men.
#
AS/SO=PL No steady relationships. Always forming new relationships. Change of associations with others. A change of social intercourse during meal times (eating with different people).
AS/PL=SO Change in the surroundings effects a transition in the body. Changes of associates.
SO/PL=AS The environmental influence on nutrition and physical development. The environmental life style and the manner in which one is influenced by it.
#
AS/SO=CU Family celebrations, betrothals and weddings. To enjoy and be active in social gatherings with others. Relationships formed with artists, families or club members.
AS/CU=SO To be a bridegroom. Landlord. Tenant of an apartment. To be invited.
SO/CU=AS To be a bride or bridegroom. Relationships to an association of artists.
#
AS/SO=HA To be disgusted with unpleasant things and to be tied to them. Acquaintance with people who are ill.
AS/HA=SO To put oneself in danger of bodily harm from others. To come bodily into contact with dirt.
SO/HA=AS To deal with the sick, as a profession. Physician. To keep secrets. To be under professional discretion. To have difficulties through acquaintances or in general.
#
AS/SO=ZE To inspire and guide other people to a goal. Interaction with creative and motivated persons. Ties also with military personnel. Fire alarm.
AS/ZE=SO To be forced to become a leader. To travel by train. Railroad passenger. To associate with railroad employees.
SO/ZE=AS Convocation (mustering troops into service). Comradship. To back others. Handling machines.
#
AS/SO=KR To be greeted by important authority figures. To appear independent. Connections to public influential personalities of high standing. Personal interaction with people in high places ... authorities.
AS/KR=SO To be a subordinate. To be obliged to execute orders.
SO/KR=AS Connection with the father. To be the boss of others. Intercourse with high and independent people.
#
AS/SO=AP Social gatherings with dignitaries and successful people. Many acquaintances are formed in the field of science or commerce. To meet many people. To interact with men of knowledge who have the answers.
AS/AP=SO To be familiar to many by appearance. To obtain honor and success through acquaintances.
SO/AP=AS Relations with successful people perhaps enjoying their protection. Acquaintance with many, with scientists and representatives of commerce and industry.
#
AS/SO=AD To stick to the same people. A dislike for making new acquaintances. Sterile relations.
AS/AD=SO To be closely joined to the place (soil or property). The native is prominent. He has an exceptional position.
SO/AD=AS To refuse connections with others. No desire for connections. Homeland. Native place.
#
AS/SO=VU To be associated with powerful and prestigious people who have a strong influence on other people.
AS/VU=SO To have to deal with bodily stronger people. Others are bodily superior. To be kept in check.
SO/VU=AS Necessity to apply physical strength. To deal with people of great physical strength.
#
AS/SO=PO Relationships with people who are spiritually guided.
AS/PO=SO Association with mentally wide awake people. To be tutored and enlightened by others.
SO/PO=AS To deal with spiritual men, to live in such an environment.
#
AS/MO=KN Closer union with female persons or the public. Connected with many people.
AS/KN=MO Acquaintances with females. Connections resulting in public.
MO/KN=AS Acquaintance with female persons.
#
AS/MO=ME Correspondence or conversation with female persons. Professional conversations with many people.
AS/ME=MO Talks about female persons. Vehicles and transport facilities in the neighborhood.
MO/ME=AS Acquaintance with the young, especially with young girls. To be with other people during business hours. To work with vehicles. Associations from youth.
#
AS/MO=VE Love affairs with female persons. Pleasant connections (general).
AS/VE=MO Nice female acquaintances. A woman's love for the native.
MO/VE=AS Love acquaintances of a woman. Acquaintance with loving women. To be together with loving women.
#
AS/MO=MA The wife. The mother. Quarrels with female persons. Common activity with others in public.
AS/MA=MO To become known in public through actions. Arguments with female persons. To exert charm of sex upon a woman.
MO/MA=AS Acquaintances with woman or with the future wife. Working relations with other people in the surroundings.
#
AS/MO=JU Good luck with female acquaintances and good luck in public.
AS/JU=MO Births. Luck through acquaintanceship with women. To find quick recognition.
MO/JU=AS Acquaintance with cheerful and serene women. Happy and cheerful surroundings.
#
AS/MO=SA Separations from female acquaintances and difficulties through them.
AS/SA=MO To take leave of women. To be separated or rejected from female persons.
MO/SA=AS Acquaintance with widows or with old, unfortunate or sick women. Hindrance, changes in the surroundings. Associating with country people. Rustic surroundings.
#
AS/MO=UR Spontaneous relationship. Accidents of female persons, aggravations through them. Rich in eventful hours with others.
AS/UR=MO Sudden unusual events with female persons.
MO/UR=AS Energetic wife. Acquaintance with an energetic woman. To be present, to witness, to experience an uproar or exciting event. To be kept under high tension by others. Surroundings are full of tension.
#
AS/MO=NE Female persons are withdrawing. Insincere women. Secrecies among female acquaintances. False, slanderous female persons.
AS/NE=MO To be influenced by others. Evil female influence. Doubtful female acquaintances, or classes of people in general. Fraudulent matters in the public.
MO/NE=AS Acquaintances or association with sensitive persons, or with virgins. Nightly association.
#
AS/MO=PL Changes which do not last. Changes of one's own circumstances through acquaintance with females. Changes in public conditions.
AS/PL=MO Environment influences on the development of character. The influence of a change in place on the mind. In other places, outside, the native appears in the public. Brought into the public by others.
MO/PL=AS Uncertain attitude toward others and the place. Coming and going. Change of place in hourly rhythm. Change of shift.
#
AS/MO=CU Social gatherings with women. Getting acquainted with the wife. The quiet hours in the home. To become a public person through unions or parties.
AS/CU=MO Social relations with female persons. To be together with many people. Houses and buildings for service to the public like railroad stations. City hall, post offices or libraries.
MO/CU=AS One's own wife as fiancee. Acquaintance with fiancees. Visitors. Leisure hours with others at home.
#
AS/MO=HA Ailments of female persons. Unpleasant things through women. Oppressive events in public.
AS/HA=MO Impoverished female persons. To expose them to danger. Acquaintance with poor old widowed or lonely woman. To become publicly known in a disagreeable manner.
MO/HA=AS Connection with worried, depressed persons. Acquaintance with prostitutes, or with bad women. Worries caused by others, or which one causes others.
#
AS/MO=ZE Pregnancy of acquainted persons. Stimulating female acquaintance. Connected with military persons or persons giving orders. To become public through one's own activity.
AS/ZE=MO To accomplish a task with others. Making plans with others. The working crew or shift in a plant.
MO/ZE=AS Others have to obey. Acquaintance with machine personnel, those working with controlled fire and/or military persons.
#
AS/MO=KR Acquaintance with female state officials or independent, self-assured women. Social relations with officials.
AS/KR=MO The husband. To be the master of a woman. Employed in public service.
MO/KR=AS Acquaintance with independent women. Dealing with officials or governments.
#
AS/MO=AP Many female acquaintances. Female customers. Many people in one place. Connected with the public through commerce and science.
AS/AP=MO To become known to the public. To have women as customers or clients.
MO/AP=AS To spend many hours together with many people.
#
AS/MO=AD Grave hindrances and resistances in public. Deaths among the resident population.
AS/AD=MO To come into the public through superiority. The settled populace. Country people. The domicile. The territory of a nation.
MO/AD=AS Death of acquaintances or in nearer surroundings, emotionally connected with them. To express sorrow, condolence.
#
AS/MO=VU Demonstration of power by female persons. To be powerfully influenced by the public in the near surroundings. To be strongly connected with the public. To be socially with representatives of the executive in one place.
AS/VU=MO Women or persons in public who tend to violence or bring their power and influence into bearing.
MO/VU=AS To cause public interest through others, or others cause public interest for the native. To experience great events with others. Being connected with others through great events and having together great influence on the public, to meet these people socially.
#
AS/MO=PO Communication with high-minded women. Under influence of the higher and finer qualities of the mind. To appear spiritualized in public.
AS/PO=MO The spiritual susceptibility of other people. To become publicly known through spiritual relations with others. Spiritualized women.
MO/PO=AS To influence others mentally. Relations to people who are susceptible to mental and spiritual things.
#
AS/KN=ME Local telephone calls. Discussion and mental association with others.
AS/ME=KN To make contact or connections by letter or conversation.
KN/ME=AS Wanting to meet. Engagement. Rendezvous.
#
AS/KN=VE Nice acquaintances and connections. Love-acquaintances. Sensual connections.
AS/VE=KN Personal love unions. Many nice connections.
KN/VE=AS Pleasant and harmonious relations. Love ties with other people.
#
AS/KN=MA Erotic relationships. Expressing wishes. Actions taken with acquaintances. To act together with others.
AS/MA=KN To enter into a sex union. To be called upon, to be asked. In connection with others under compulsion (an irresistible impulse to act ... compelled).
KN/MA=AS Common activity with others. Sex union, personal.
#
AS/KN=JU Fortunate acquaintances. Useful connections show results.
AS/JU=KN Good relations with other people.
KN/JU=AS Good connections with others.
#
AS/KN=SA No contact with the closer environment. Separating from such contacts.
AS/SA=KN To be present at separations.
KN/SA=AS Breaking off of unions. No contact with one's environment.
#
AS/KN=UR Sudden acquaintances, and to experience excitement through them.
AS/UR=KN To want an acquaintance. Others want to get acquainted with the native.
KN/UR=AS Suddenly meet or make acquaintance.
#
AS/KN=NE To be wrongly connected. Dissolving of connections. Being deceived and kept in uncertainty by them.
AS/NE=KN To be embroiled in fraudulent activities.
KN/NE=AS To be deserted by others. Not able to find a following. Others are being deserted.
#
AS/KN=PL To prepare the way for new acquaintances. Widening of connections. Change through a relationship. New connections through changes at the place.
AS/PL=KN To form new connections in the place.
KN/PL=AS Changes in the relations or connections cause changes in or of the place where you live.
#
AS/KN=CU Connections with artists, with people who are in the same house, with the family, with clubs, unions etc.
AS/CU=KN Acquaintances through family members. To establish sociable connections. Partner in a society or to partake in sociability. Dealing with matters concerning flats and apartments.
KN/CU=AS Betrothal. To be invited. To live with other people.
#
AS/KN=HA Disagreeable acquaintances. Misunderstandings through them. To avoid certain acquaintances and certain places. Closely connected persons suffer from sorrow and illness.
AS/HA=KN To be entangled.
KN/HA=AS Secret connection With others. To be visited by criminals, burglars. To be fed up with other people.
#
AS/KN=ZE Known persons get excited, they deal with or work with fire. Working with others. Connections with creative persons.
AS/ZE=KN To accomplish something with others.
KN/ZE=AS To commit something with several others. To be armed together with others.
#
AS/KN=KR Having connections with people higher up or very self-confident persons. Personal relations to members of the government. To be an adherent.
AS/KR=KN To be received by independent people. To be engaged to work. To enter a position.
KN/KR=AS Employment-contract. Others have difficulties with the authorities. To become entangled with the authorities through others. The beginning or end of a lawsuit.
#
AS/KN=AP Relations with business people and trades people in the near environment. General business connections or relations, also acquaintances with scientists and customers. Expanded limits of the immediate environment.
AS/AP=KN To make contact with customers. Many customers. The representative.
KN/AP=AS Union of free and equal people. Economic or scientific connections with others. (States that are united).
#
AS/KN=AD Hindrances and obstacles through acquaintances. A small circle of acquaintances which always remains the same.
AS/AD=KN The relationship with others who are overcome by the native. The connection with the place where one stays.
KN/AD=AS To be home, to be on the estate. To stay in touch with home. Others are inferior and can't develop.
#
AS/KN=VU influence through local connections. To be allied with influential people.
AS/VU=KN To use violent people, to keep in touch with them.
KN/VU=AS To be eager. The will to jointly accomplish something. To become enthusiastic about something together with others. To be in eagerness and excitement together with others. Others are mightily excited. Others are in excitement.
#
AS/KN=PO Connections with people spiritually developed. To gain a growing understanding through such acquaintances.
AS/PO=KN To keep connections with other people for mental reasons.
KN/PO=AS To be spiritually associated with others.
#
AS/ME=VE Exchange of thoughts about love. To find friendly thinking.
AS/VE=ME Agreeable acquaintances in the close surroundings. To amuse oneself with other people (to talk sweet nothings).
ME/VE=AS To meet with friends and new people. The art of being adaptable.
#
AS/ME=MA Dispute with others. To be critically and sharply judged.
AS/MA=ME To quarrel with people verbally or by letter. To be obliged to reply to others. Arguments.
ME/MA=AS To negotiate with others. Debate.
#
AS/ME=JU To enjoy a good reputation. To be well thought of by others. To be hilarious with others.
AS/JU=ME To converse pleasantly with others.
ME/JU=AS Happy and cheerful entertainment and conversation.
#
AS/ME=SA Farewell visits to depart. To be denied, refused.
AS/SA=ME To be occupied with thoughts of separation.
ME/SA=AS To take leave. To depart or go away. To move. To travel. To roam.
#
AS/ME=UR Others get excited and talk excited. To be involved in an argument. Altercation.
AS/UR=ME To receive a message suddenly.
ME/UR=AS To be asked suddenly by other people.
#
AS/ME=NE To discuss unclear matters. To be told lies by others. Evil gossip.
AS/NE=ME To commit fraud with other people. Others deceive through smart talk or lies. Language was given to men in order that they cover up their thoughts.".
ME/NE=AS To be cheated by acquaintances. To be outsmarted.
#
AS/ME=PL The opinions about the native undergo change. Others draw their consequences or conclusions.
AS/PL=ME To think of changes in the location. News about changes of location.
ME/PL=AS To have dealings with adaptable people. To speak about a changed situation with others. Others change their views.
#
AS/ME=CU Intentions of marriage. Domestic themes of conversation or conversations at home. Talks about marriage, social conditions or associations.
AS/CU=ME The thoughts are on the home, or the parents' home. To receive an invitation to a social.
ME/CU=AS Socially conversing with others. Social intercourse with artists.
#
AS/ME=HA Others are mistaken. Evil gossip. Slanderous talk, calumniation concerning oneself.
AS/HA=ME To speak about evil things. To plot mean acts with others. To receive bad news.
ME/HA=AS Defamation. To be threatened by others.
#
AS/ME=ZE To give commands or orders. To have to obey. Talks about machines or technical matters.
AS/ZE=ME The native's way is shown to him. The thoughts are forced to follow a fixed trend. To be under the influence of other's opinions. To be compelled to much conversation. Talks about machines.
ME/ZE=AS To be asked. To ask others.
#
AS/ME=KR The native enjoys high regard by others. To converse about great or important matters. To give advice to others.
AS/KR=ME Orders by superiors. To carry out orders. To direct one's thoughts according to others. To have no right to an opinion of one's own. Language courses. Arbitrated decision.
ME/KR=AS Tied to others through great ideas. Acquaintance with thinkers and poets.
#
AS/ME=AP Scientific or business talks, arguments, actions, deals.
AS/AP=ME Business agreements. Talks with customers. To wait on them.
ME/AP=AS Many talks with others. Acquaintanceship with scientists.
#
AS/ME=AD Serious and deep talks. To say very little, to save words, to be taciturn.
AS/AD=ME Unable to follow the thoughts of the native. A lack of logic. Thoughts, opinions, talks about the residence or the place.
ME/AD=AS Intercourse with people who sit much, like off ice personnel or any crew of a vehicle.
#
AS/ME=VU To be very much occupied with professional or business talks. To be hurried.
AS/VU=ME Others are superior in thinking. To be strongly influenced by the opinion of others. Others don't permit the native to have a free opinion.
ME/VU=AS To be with people who are much on the move.
#
AS/ME=PO To be connected with other people by thoughts and ideas.
AS/PO=ME Union of ideas with others. To be brought together with others for the same idea.
ME/PO=AS To meet or to have dealings with people who represent an idea.
#
AS/VE=MA To be the object of desire.
AS/MA=VE Sex union. Intimate relations.
VE/MA=AS Personal sexual intercourse. Acquaintance with relatives or intimate friends.
#
AS/VE=JU To be the object of love's happiness. Fortunate and agreeable experiences with others. Nice conversations with others. Necking and petting.
AS/JU=VE To get help and assistance. To be loved by many.
VE/JU=AS Pleasantness and success through others. To be admired.
#
AS/VE=SA To be deserted. Disturbances and interruptions in love-affairs.
AS/SA=VE Love's separation.
VE/SA=AS To hinder the inclinations of others. To quit others. To be forsaken.
#
AS/VE=UR Suddenly in love. Surprising experience of sympathy and goodwill.
AS/UR=VE To fall in love suddenly.
VE/UR=AS Flirtations. Betrothal or marriage. Excitement about the love of others.
#
AS/VE=NE To be deceived in love or cheated. To be made to believe that one is treated well. Separation of harmonious relations.
AS/NE=VE To be deceived in love.
VE/NE=AS Wrong love mate. Tied to an unhappy love. Acquaintance with people suffering from unfortunate love.
#
AS/VE=PL To make oneself popular ... to be likeable. Increase of popularity. Transformations and changes through loving acquaintances.
AS/PL=VE To desire a change in the place. Nice change in the surroundings. To think of the beautification of the environment.
VE/PL=AS The beginning of a pleasant change in the environment. Friendlier surroundings.
#
AS/VE=CU Social acquaintances. To be desired in marriage.
AS/CU=VE Love acquaintances lead to marriage. Harmonious community with others. To beautify or to decorate the home. To keep the home in good order.
VE/CU=AS To be desired for marriage by many. Others wish the personal friendship of the native.
#
AS/VE=HA Difficulties or restrictions in love or other pleasant connections. To be the object of mercenary or illicit love. Servants and people socially below the native show their affection.
AS/HA=VE To be tied to others by vulgar love. Sympathy with people who live in the slums.
VE/HA=AS Deal with people with vulgar manners. Tied to others through low love.
#
AS/VE=ZE Paternally desired. To desire motherhood. "The favored one." Pleasant and creative connections.
AS/ZE=VE To comply with a request or desire of others. To bestow love. To create progeny.
VE/ZE=AS To be impregnated. Others are forced to settle peacefully, to make peace, or the native is forced to make peace.
#
AS/VE=KR To be desired as husband, as head of the family. To be liked and loved by superiors. To gain advancement through good acquaintances.
AS/KR=VE To be liked in the capacity of an official or an employee. To be the favorite.
VE/KR=AS Acquaintances with charitable people. To receive presents.
#
AS/VE=AP Good standing in relationships with friends. To be loved by many.
AS/AP=VE To be well liked as a customer.
VE/AP=AS To be around faithful and reliable people.
#
AS/VE=AD Harmonious community in a small circle. To pass by the affections and love of other people. Loved by only a few.
AS/AD=VE To be in harmony with the place. Others feel their inferiority. Servility.
VE/AD=AS Respect and sympathy from a few.
#
AS/VE=VU To be greatly loved by others. "Tell me with whom you associate and I'll tell you who you are.".
AS/VU=VE To enjoy great popularity with others. To feel well in one's surroundings.
VE/VU=AS To be powerfully attracted by others or to attract others powerfully.
#
AS/VE=PO Spiritual or religious harmony with others. Souls in harmony.
AS/PO=VE Religious acquaintances. To prefer people who are religiously inclined.
VE/PO=AS Acquaintance with priests, preachers etc.
#
AS/MA=JU To be forced by others into one's own good fortune. To become engaged or betrothed. Other people are successful. A�antages and profits through the action, deeds or accomplishments of others.
AS/JU=MA To receive proposals. To gain a willing ear. Betrothal. To receive orders.
MA/JU=AS The betrothal. A vow or pledge. Taking the bath.
#
AS/MA=SA Through actions of others to be separated from something. To suffer losses. To participate in funeral rites.
AS/SA=MA Compelled to do depressing work. Disgusted with work. Slow to make up one's mind..slowed action. Death. Obsequies.
MA/SA=AS Actions of others which bring sorrow. Cases of illness. Bereavement. Death in the family, or of acquaintances.
#
AS/MA=UR Sudden or surprising actions of other people and excitement through them. To be injured suddenly.
AS/UR=MA Surprising deeds of others. To be injured physically by others.
MA/UR=AS To be connected with sudden events. To rebel together with others, or others rebel against the native.
#
AS/MA=NE Thoughtless deeds of others. Deceit and disappointment through the deeds of others. Danger of infection. Danger of destruction.
AS/NE=MA To be damaged. To be infected by other people.
MA/NE=AS Rejection of acquaintances. To be infected by other persons. Deaths.
#
AS/MA=PL To plan common activities. Changes through the actions of others. Preparations for moving or change of place.
AS/PL=MA To plan changes.
MA/PL=AS To plan with others. To have something in mind.
#
AS/MA=CU Acting in union with others. The wedding of other people. Co-operation with an artist or of artists.
AS/CU=MA Wanting to marry. Common activities with family members. Housekeeping work. Homework.
MA/CU=AS Common interest groups. A personal tie leads to marriage.
#
AS/MA=HA Obstacles because of the deeds of others. To be the victim of theft. Others act wrongly. To adjust the mistakes of others. To be in danger of losing one's life. Participating in robberies.
AS/HA=MA Assisting others in case of accidents. Ineffective faulty deeds, and faulty actions of others. To be threatened by others.
MA/HA=AS Vulgar and evil acts of others. To have to set right the mistakes of others. Danger of being murdered, also danger of other people being murdered.
#
AS/MA=ZE To be active as a producer. Others bring pressure to bear on the situation. Injuries by fire or through fire-arms.
AS/ZE=MA To be forced to a deed. Others enforce a decision. To receive burns. To be injured by somebody.
MA/ZE=AS Energetic action of others, or against others Acquaintance with soldiers, officers, machinists, or technicians. To be threatened by quick firing weapons. Conflicts.
#
AS/MA=KR To be punished by the authorities.
AS/KR=MA To have to act independent as employee or official. Executive official. To do physical labor for others.
MA/KR=AS To bring about autocratic deeds with others. Acquaintance with people who are independent or act autocratically. To be threatened by the government. To be arrested.
#
AS/MA=AP Common scientific or economic activities with others.
AS/AP=MA Business activities. To receive business orders. To be commissioned for research.
MA/AP=AS To be active with others in trade, Commerce or science. Working together with others in trade and science.
#
AS/MA=AD To be depressed or oppressed by others.
AS/AD=MA Unsuccessful actions of others, Others act from a feeling of inferiority.
MA/AD=AS To retire from work. To cease co-operation.
#
AS/MA=VU Others act on their own authority. Violent deeds of others. To be exposed to grave threats of others.
AS/VU=MA Others have the power of disposal. " A tough guy." Acrobats.
MA/VU=AS Powerful activity of others. Powerful activity with others. Others are head and shoulders above the rest. Mighty preponderance of others. Possibility of becoming violent to others and of receiving violence from them.
#
AS/MA=PO The moral behavior of other people. Luck with other people; or other people's luck (fortune). To experience good deeds from others.
AS/PO=MA Separation of minds. To separate from others as a result of their morals.
MA/PO=AS Moral attitude of others. Intellectual activity with others. To be under the intellectual influence of others or to influence others intellectually, ethically or morally.
#
AS/JU=SA To experience refusal. Not being accepted. Separations from friends.
AS/SA=JU Separation with fortunate ending. Deserted by co-tenants. Change of residence.
JU/SA=AS Separation from a person. Change of residence.
#
AS/JU=UR Sudden luck with others or through others.
AS/UR=JU Happiness (good fortune) with or of other people. Financial advantage through them.
JU/UR=AS Happy companionship.
#
AS/JU=NE To play games or to speculate with others. To lose money through others. Seeming happiness with others.
AS/NE=JU To commit money frauds.
JU/NE=AS Money easily earned through others. Games of chance. To win or lose money in connection with others. Participants in a lottery. Betting.
#
AS/JU=PL To experience increasing benevolence from others. Good adaptability to the environment. Happy change with others.
AS/PL=JU Fortunate changes at the place, or of the place.
JU/PL=AS Pleasant change of the surroundings, of the place or in the place.
#
AS/JU=CU Wedding guests. Harmony in the family. One's own peaceful home is one's paradise.
AS/CU=JU Home comfort. To be satisfied with the apartment. To be jolly with other people.
JU/CU=AS To be united in happy marriage. Fortunate partnership of others. Acquaintances through art.
#
AS/JU=HA Friendly relations to single women. Money losses.
AS/HA=JU Others in need of money.
JU/HA=AS To be impoverished by others. Other people are the cause of less income.
#
AS/JU=ZE Fatherhood. To advance through patronage in one's own work.
AS/ZE=JU Good things are forced upon us. To be forced by others into fortune or luck. To create pleasure with others.
JU/ZE=AS To work with others.
#
AS/JU=KR Harmonious relations with the father, with the boss or with the state.
AS/KR=JU Trustee for money. Cashier.
JU/KR=AS Social Intercourse with leading persons.
#
AS/JU=AP To be fortunate with many. Good relations with business people or men of science.
AS/AP=JU Solvent customers. To be fortunate with business people.
JU/AP=AS Tied to others by science or commerce.
#
AS/JU=AD Luck and fortune through being settled. To seek one's happiness in a peaceful environment.
AS/AD=JU To acclimatize oneself fast. Fortunate home surroundings. To find one's luck and happiness there. Other people are envious. Rivalry.
JU/AD=AS A quiet, secluded, retiring place or location. To spend more money than others. To be envied by others. Others lack money.
#
AS/JU=VU Luck through mighty enterprises, or through enterprising and jolly people.
AS/VU=JU Others use all their power for the best of the native. To be infected by optimism of others.
JU/VU=AS To instill hope and trust in others, to give them courage to go on. To be influenced by others.
#
AS/JU=PO In the company of prudent and reasonable men.
AS/PO=JU Friends who get advice, counsel, understanding and wisdom from the native.
JU/PO=AS To deal with reasonable and intelligent people. The rights of others. Treated fair by others.
#
AS/SA=UR Sudden leave-taking.
AS/UR=SA To separate suddenly from others.
SA/UR=AS Grave misunderstandings with others. Sudden separation from other people.
#
AS/SA=NE To be separated through falsehood or lies.
AS/NE=SA To be defrauded by others. Damages or obstacles through the refusal of others.
SA/NE=AS Infection of other people. To be infected. To vaccinate.
#
AS/SA=PL To put up with increasing disrespect and repression. Others effect a separation. Slow separation from others.
AS/PL=SA Separation from the environment. Changes of the place with difficulties. Isolation.
SA/PL=AS Others fit themselves, suit themselves, only very slowly. Slow separation from others. The beginning of a separation which lasts for some time. Relations to quite different persons.
#
AS/SA=CU Separation in the family or from the dwelling.
AS/CU=SA Dissolution of betrothal and marriage. Separation from the family, the apartment or a union.
SA/CU=AS Not belonging to an association or community, to keep apart from others, not wanting any community with others, odd person, crank. Separation from others.
#
AS/SA=HA To be tied to sorrowful people. To be separated by a serious illness. Hostility.
AS/HA=SA Big disagreeableness. To be separated from others by accident, poverty or illness.
SA/HA=AS To come into touch with the seriously sick. To be around suppressed, persecuted, refugee people.
#
AS/SA=ZE Separation from the father or from the parents.
AS/ZE=SA Hindrances, obstacles forced upon us by others. Other people enforce a discharge (job). Discharged from the armi. Losses by fire or machinery.
SA/ZE=AS Machines damaged. To be endangered by fire. Fellowmen in the fire, in war.
#
AS/SA=KR To be separated from the father or from the boss. Separations from other people by the power of the state.
AS/KR=SA As official or employee severely handicapped. To reject the boss or the government. To get notice. Change of employment.
SA/KR=AS To be associated with impoverished corporations. No payment from the state or boss.
#
AS/SA=AP Frequently obliged to take leave of others.
AS/AP=SA Separation from customers. Business connections are severed. To have pupils. Interruption of schooling.
SA/AP=AS Others separate from a community. To be separated from many.
#
AS/SA=AD To be cornered by other people. To be crowded by local conditions.
AS/AD=SA Others feel conscious of guilt. To feel limited and depressed through the domicile.
SA/AD=AS To get into trouble through others. Others are in trouble or forced position.
#
AS/SA=VU To be kept under close observation. To be guarded.
AS/VU=SA Others are in dire need, are restrained or restrain the native or meet with powerful resistance.
SA/VU=AS Others must be attentive to their advantage. To be guarded. To be imprisoned.
#
AS/SA=PO Mental separation. To be refused and separated mentally. To be or to become separated in matters of world-concept.
AS/PO=SA Others mentally draw the dividing line. Others hinder mentally. They are trying to teach you.
SA/PO=AS To be a model to others mentally. Another person is a model to us. To be mentally separated from others in one's outlook on life. To be different.
#
AS/UR=NE To depart this life; to be separated from others through death.
AS/NE=UR Others do not see things clearly. Sudden events brought about by others. To be involved by others through lack of understanding.
UR/NE=AS Other people do not know what they want. They are pro and con and try to create difficulties and disturbances. Notices of death. Acquaintance with dead people.
#
AS/UR=PL Sudden new acquaintances. Surprising changes in or of the place.
AS/PL=UR Sudden surprising changes of the place.
UR/PL=AS Sudden changes of the place, the view of the place, as for instance scenic view obstructed through new buildings.
#
AS/UR=CU To get married suddenly. Exciting events in the home.
AS/CU=UR A surprise marriage. Sudden event in a marriage or at a wedding. Excitement in the family. To surprisingly obtain an apartment. Modern home. Excitement in or through a community.
UR/CU=AS Sudden weddings of others. To find oneself suddenly in a different company.
#
AS/UR=HA Lowness of others. Danger of assassination. To be accessory to a killing.
AS/HA=UR Sudden hatefulness or disagreeableness. Danger of assassination. To lose one's life suddenly, also others loose their life suddenly.
UR/HA=AS To have to deal with low class people. Obliged to make oneself responsible for ugly deeds of others. Assassination of well-known persons. Others commit wicked deeds, the native has to set matters straight.
#
AS/UR=ZE To be under the compulsion of others. To be an incendiary (on fire).
AS/ZE=UR Others are in excitement because of or through the native. Danger to the native from explosions, lightning or machines.
UR/ZE=AS To be under the will power of others. To be submissive to others. Losses through flashes of lightning.
#
AS/UR=KR To be implicated in an event by the state. The execution of orders.
AS/KR=UR Sudden scenes with superiors.
UR/KR=AS Great excitement caused by the native. To be detained by the power of the State, or to perish by if.
#
AS/UR=AP Others show great interest for the native. Acquaintances who are interested in astrology. Astrological customers.
AS/AP=UR Sudden expansion in business. To be acquainted with new or revolutionary ideas in the field of science.
UR/AP=AS Others are under tension, in cheerful excitement and expectation.
#
AS/UR=AD Sudden and definite quitting, and moving away. To become unsettled, to be frightened up from a comfortable rest.
AS/AD=UR Excitements and quarrels with others because they feel inferior. To quit a place for a while. To be absent for a time. Matters of location cause conflict and suddenly appearing enmities.
UR/AD=AS Sudden breaking off of connections. Others break off the connections with the native. The definite end of connections. Danger of death or murder from others or for others.
#
AS/UR=VU Intensified excitement of other people in the home or at a certain location.
AS/VU=UR Others become suddenly engaged in close fights, come to blows, act suddenly, explosively, abruptly.
UR/VU=AS To be suddenly exposed to violent acts of others or with others. To live in a dangerous environment. Danger through current energies, through technical medium.
#
AS/UR=PO Enlightenment and propaganda.
AS/PO=UR Others suddenly come to an understanding ... realization.
UR/PO=AS To instill ideas in others, to stimulate them mentally or to be stimulated. To spread enlightenment and propaganda together with others. Showing motion pictures to a community.
#
AS/NE=PL Underhanded tricks and fraud which are not noticed. To give something up because one was misled by others. To be outwitted.
AS/PL=NE To have to give up something through change of the surroundings. Change of place, where the results or consequences are only noticed much later, or not at all.
NE/PL=AS To quit a matter.
#
AS/NE=CU To be united in unhappy marriage. To wish to quit a union. Secrecies in the home. To be deceived about the true conditions in the home.
AS/CU=NE Dissolved betrothal or society. Divorce. The parents disappoint. Dissolution, breaking up of the parents' home. Shortage of living quarters.
NE/CU=AS To be unmarried. To enter into a precarious marital union. A partnership which soon again is dissolved. Unpleasant housing facilities.
#
AS/NE=HA To be the cause of a drowning. To get into danger through others. To sustain damages.
AS/HA=NE Corruption and decline of the environment. Death by drowning.
NE/HA=AS Others feel deceived and disappointed To be deceived about the relations to the near surroundings. Sickness or death by water or dropsy.
#
AS/NE=ZE Others try in vain to usurp leadership. To be scalded by other people.
AS/ZE=NE To be without progeny due to marriage partner. Others incite you without success.
NE/ZE=AS Unsuccessful activities with others. To be forced to mix with people who are show-offs (pompous person, braggart). Scalded by others.
#
AS/NE=KR To reject authorities because one feels oneself deceived. To be the leader or to follow a leader in fraudulent enterprises.
AS/KR=NE To give up employment. Quitting a business by the principal. Bankruptcy of the employer. To be deceived by the state.
NE/KR=AS To be involved in the dissolution of an establishment. In touch with unpleasantness.
#
AS/NE=AP To be deceived many times. To gain ample experience through fraud and tricks.
AS/AP=NE Lack of business confidence. Bad credit risk. Unreliable customers.
NE/AP=AS Embezzlement or deception. The truth is brought to light.
#
AS/NE=AD To pay much attention not to be deceived or defrauded.
AS/AD=NE To have the wrong impression about a place. Others hide their inferiority. Giving up the residence.
NE/AD=AS Others experience changes in their economic situation. Change in relation to the environment or to other people.
#
AS/NE=VU Mighty deception and disappointment through others.
AS/VU=NE Others waste their efforts, forced to learn a lesson. Others succumb by a trial of strength.
NE/VU=AS To experience through others a great deception or disappointment. To be deprived through others of influence and power.
#
AS/NE=PO To have to deal with visionary, deluded or mentally blind people.
AS/PO=NE Others are mentally or spiritually led astray or they are characterized by the higher and finer qualities of the mind.
NE/PO=AS Others who follow the wrong faith, who advocate the erroneous faith.
#
AS/PL=CU Changes in or of the home. Change in the social relations.
AS/CU=PL Change in social relations.
PL/CU=AS Change in social conditions, relations. To experience with others the transformation of a social structure.
#
AS/PL=HA Deficiency prevents change. Degradation of the local situations.
AS/HA=PL To fail together with others. Change in a sad surroundings. (To improve with a good, or to get worse with a poor PL).
PL/HA=AS Others withdraw from an association because of inability. Dealing with people who cannot cope with the appearing difficulties, who fail in the struggle for life.
#
AS/PL=ZE New duties. Change of place for enlargement of sphere of activities or changes at the place which enable expansion of work facilities.
AS/ZE=PL To be forced by others to work or accomplish more.
PL/ZE=AS Others have to increase efforts, or others force the native to increase efforts. Competition.
#
AS/PL=KR Change of place for the purpose of training or educating pupils. Change of pupils. Increasing demands.
AS/KR=PL To be an apprentice. Change in the relation to the chief.
PL/KR=AS To experience through others or with others a great change. Great changes at the place. To attend an educational school with others.
#
AS/PL=AP Many changes in the location. Elaborate changes in the place. Change of location for many. Many travel.
AS/AP=PL Changing customer or business connections. Transient customers.
PL/AP=AS Others cause a large change in the life of the native. Beginning of a big change at the place.
#
AS/PL=AD Deep reaching change in relation to others.
AS/AD=PL Changes in the place. Others begin to feel inferior.
PL/AD=AS The native goes through deep reaching changes in relation to the surroundings and other people.
#
AS/PL=VU Changes of the place in connection with important or decisive changes in the life of the native.
AS/VU=PL Changes through violent interference of others. To experience a quick change through the influence of others.
PL/VU=AS Great transformations in the surroundings experienced with others.
#
AS/PL=PO Changes of place for the purpose of intellectual and spiritual development and education.
AS/PO=PL To be with people who go through mental or spiritual transformations.
PL/PO=AS The people around the native change and develop mentally.
#
AS/CU=HA Bereavement in the family. To have a poor marriage-partner. Poor family environment. Unhealthy places. Scarceness of apartments.
AS/HA=CU To experience disagreeable things through or in communities. To commit adultery, or adultery committed by others. Acquaintances with poor artists or vulgar art.
CU/HA=AS Miserable marriage bond. To be unhappy in one's own home. Disharmony through home conditions. The home life is not social. Cellar warehouse (store-room for provision). Sickness in the family.
#
AS/CU=ZE Increase in the family. Jointly striving for a goal. New buildings. To gain additional living quarters. Neighborhood.
AS/ZE=CU Unity is power. Community work in art.
CU/ZE=AS Common enterprises with others. Stockholder in a corporation.
#
AS/CU=KR To be married by civil authority. Belonging to a community by order of state. Living quarters provided by the state. Great mansion.
AS/KR=CU Organization of employees or officials. Guild or labor union. To abide by marriage laws of the country. To be an art student or employed in a house or home.
CU/KR=AS To meet with distinguished persons. To be connected with great artists. To take part in weddings.
#
AS/CU=AP With many people in a community. Urban manner of construction. Many people walk in and out. Many apartments in a house. To have tenants or to be a tenant. Large tenement house.
AS/AP=CU Door to door customers. Tenant.
CU/AP=AS To be connected with other people through art, science or family relations.
#
AS/CU=AD A small family or community. The one-family house, country style (rustic).
AS/AD=CU The father house. The place of the family. Art located at a special place. Others are envious. Others feel inferior in the family, in a community or in exercising art.
CU/AD=AS The members of an association drift apart, they seclude themselves.
#
AS/CU=VU The power through the same direction of will.
AS/VU=CU Others show their allied power.
CU/VU=AS Others bring their power to bear by their community. Great esteem or respect for the artistic accomplishments of others. Great artistic creation together with others. The friendship or company of great artists.
#
AS/CU=PO Consecrated places, churches or chapels, parsonages, vicarages. Family relations with priests or ministers.
AS/PO=CU Spiritually interested members in the family. Together with men of the same world-concept.
CU/PO=AS In the company of people who represent a faith, religion.
#
AS/HA=ZE Constraint. To assist in creating incendiaries (fires), or to be threatened by such. To be exposed to oppression.
AS/ZE=HA Spiteful or vulgar deeds of others. Damage through fire by other persons.
HA/ZE=AS Others are obnoxious, hateful and negative, disorderly or lazy. Disorder and dirt in the surroundings. Damage by fire.
#
AS/HA=KR Great lack and damage in the near environment. To cause damage to the state or to independent persons in company with others.
AS/KR=HA To be dismissed. To lose employment. Austerity by order of the state. To be impoverished by the state. To go to prison.
HA/KR=AS Great damage to the native through meanness of others. To be involved in serious misfortune.
#
AS/HA=AP Expansion of poverty and need. Dealing with poor or disagreeable customers. An authority on antiques. Nuisance through beggars.
AS/AP=HA Poor or unpleasant customers. To do business with matters that are evil. Antique business or dealer in antiques.
HA/AP=AS Others are poverty-stricken. To experience mass misery in the environment. Others blame the misery on the past. Ugly things of the past are pointed out to the native.
#
AS/HA=AD Oppressed by disagreeable environment. Places or other people who suffer consistently through want and poverty.
AS/AD=HA Disagreeableness in the intimate environment. Others are the cause of their own ruin. Losses through their own defects or through their inferiority.
HA/AD=AS Connected with people, who are in need. To suffer weather change or cold.
#
AS/HA=VU To live in dangerous surroundings.
AS/VU=HA Meanness and baseness in actions of others.
HA/VU=AS Together with others under heavy oppression or impressed by violent acts. Vile acts of others. In the surroundings are people of malicious and mean character. To be implicated through others in filthy matters. Together with others in great peril.
#
AS/HA=PO Keeping company with mentally mediocre people. To be connected with others through secret sciences. Low spiritual standard of the neighborhood.
AS/PO=HA To be with others who are mentally below the native, who are mediocre. Together with stupid men.
HA/PO=AS The native lives his own life mentally in an environment which does not understand him. Others isolate themselves mentally from the native. The native seems to be incomprehensible, mentally not understood.
#
AS/ZE=KR To be stimulated to action by superiors.
AS/KR=ZE To be forced to increased activity as employee. Compulsory work. Railroad official. To be employed in a mechanical establishment. Conscription summons (drafted into service). To be called in a case of war.
ZE/KR=AS To be connected with great producers. To get into connection with armi leaders. Others take (seize) the lead.
#
AS/ZE=AP Scientific activity together with others, also industrial. Scientific creations with others.
AS/AP=ZE Things or objects that come from a distance into our closer environment. To deal with military of technical matters. Gun shop.
ZE/AP=AS To receive technical and practical training with or through others. Handling of fireworks, machines, apparatus or other technical things. Technical schools for arms and war.
#
AS/ZE=AD To be forced to conquer obstacles and opposition. To be forced to start, commence, or to take up. Others are confronted with obstacles and strong resistance.
AS/AD=ZE Bad performance of others. To be afraid of a start.
ZE/AD=AS To experience the surroundings changed along with other people. Facing different problems and to find a solution for these problems.
#
AS/ZE=VU Others enforce something. To deal with bodily superior men. To handle mechanical powers.
AS/VU=ZE Others carry their point and direct their power and superiority at a certain aim.
ZE/VU=AS To concentrate, to center all efforts for one purpose in cooperation with others. To direct the efforts of others to one goal.
#
AS/ZE=PO To be forced to a mental attitude. To be lead mentally or spiritually by other people.
AS/PO=ZE Purposeful spiritual guidance by others.
ZE/PO=AS To be mentally influenced and impressed by others. To be under the mental guidance of others. Others show their conviction and mental attitude. They are under mental compulsion.
#
AS/KR=AP Scientifically employed Assistant.
AS/AP=KR Noble customers. Attending a technical institute. Student.
KR/AP=AS To be superior to others in ability, knowledge, or in experience. To be higher rated than others. To be in the company of great scientific authorities.
#
AS/KR=AD Disagreeable subordinates. Discharged by the State.
AS/AD=KR The capital. Seat of government. Opposition to the chief, the superior or the government.
KR/AD=AS To be noted through one's odd character. To seclude oneself from others or to be secluded. Acquaintance with odd persons. Also to lead a secluded odd life together with others.
#
AS/KR=VU The competency of an official or employee. Official or employee with unrestricted power. Ambassador or secretary of state.
AS/VU=KR To have to deal with people who exercise the power of the state, to feel their power.
KR/VU=AS To be exposed to great powers and great forces. Others make their power felt or are themselves subject to the effect of the power of the State.
#
AS/KR=PO As an official or employee dealing with intellectual or spiritual things.
AS/PO=KR To have to act ideologically in compliance with others. To be with people who are mentally superior.
KR/PO=AS To be connected with others through high minded things. Others are high minded or the native appears high minded. To be under the mental influence of others.
#
AS/AP=AD Retarded business. Steady customers. No further business can be done.
AS/AD=AP Many people have a feeling of inferiority and look up to the native.
AP/AD=AS To live in a peaceful and tranquil environment. Others express their contentment. The native appears content to others.
#
AS/AP=VU Business success, expansive trade, rapidly moving merchandise.
AS/VU=AP Others back the native with success. To achieve great successes and advantages through others.
AP/VU=AS To experience success with or through others. Acquaintance with successful persons.
#
AS/AP=PO To listen. To have listeners.
AS/PO=AP To enlarge and widen the mental horizon together with many others.
AP/PO=AS People of the surroundings show their disposition and attitude.
#
AS/AD=VU The respect and rating one has at the place. Others are told to keep within bounds. To take one's chance in a trial of strength or showdown.
AS/VU=AD Desire for predominance of others meets with heavy resistance.
AD/VU=AS The native looks hard and u�ielding to others, or others look that way to him.
#
AS/AD=PO Others feel mentally inferior and want to be instructed by the native.
AS/PO=AD To go with others very deep into the bottom of a matter or issue.
AD/PO=AS In company with educated persons or persons who have a position in a school, educational or cultural system. Educational influence upon others. Training and education together with others.
#
AS/VU=PO To be under the mighty influence of others.
AS/PO=VU The tremendous mental influence of another person. Others are subject to influence.
VU/PO=AS Mutual mighty mental influences. To impress others or to be impressed.
#
SO/MO=KN Meeting with others. Effecting a friendship. Getting acquainted.
SO/KN=MO Mass meetings. A personal tie with a woman. Public relations. To establish a friendship.
MO/KN=SO The union of the woman with the man. The daily being together of man and woman.
#
SO/MO=ME The train of thoughts during the hours of the day. Intimate conversations with others. Friendship or thoughts of marriage. Interesting days and hours. Young friendships.
SO/ME=MO The young mate (marriage partner). The young body of a woman. To think of women. The male who has relationships with women at an early age.
MO/ME=SO Brain, nerves and glandular functions. A man's young wife. The schedule. The daily plan. The daily office hours.
#
SO/MO=VE A friendship of love. A marriage of love. Husband and wife in harmony. Peaceful hours.
SO/VE=MO Beautiful body of a woman. To be in love. Expressions of love.
MO/VE=SO Woman's love for the man. The man who is favored by women. The mother. Daily repeating harmonious hours.
#
SO/MO=MA Marriage. Wedlock. The act of marriage. A marriage of reproduction. A sexual friendship. A sex relationship. One's daily work.
SO/MA=MO The woman's husband. The active hours of the day.
MO/MA=SO The time of work during the day. The daily work. An active man. The man's wife.
#
SO/MO=JU Days of good luck. Fortunate experiences take place daily and hourly. Fortunate friendships. A fortunate marriage. The increase in the family. Child-birth.
SO/JU=MO The bridegroom. A fortunate and happy husband. A lucky day for women. The contented population. Choosing the right moment.
MO/JU=SO The daily repetition of happy hours. A man's happy woman.
#
SO/MO=SA Serious and difficult times. Not a good time to marry. obstacles to marriage. Sorrowful marital states. Sudden enmity in friendship and marriage. Divorce and separation.
SO/SA=MO To be without a mate. A man separated from a woman. The divorced man. Separations from females.
MO/SA=SO Divorced woman. The man's widow. Daily repeating hours of being alone. To be moody or depressed because of general personal or bodily conditions.
#
SO/MO=UR Exciting and trying days. Daily tension in partnership and marriage. Sudden events in a partnership. A clash with a friend or mate. Sudden hostility or separation.
SO/UR=MO Sudden events that take place to women. The exciting course of the day.
MO/UR=SO Energetic woman. The man-defying woman. To lead an active life. An eventful hour of the day.
#
SO/MO=NE Days of uncertainty. Cooling off in partnerships, friendships or marriage because of deceptive situation, misunderstandings or lies. The denial of a partnership or a marriage. To ultimately separate.
SO/NE=MO The divorced man. Death of the husband. To be dependent on mood or feeling. A weak husband.
MO/NE=SO The relaxed or sleeping body. Physical sensitivity. Mediumistic state.
#
SO/MO=PL The course of the day. Daily absorption of food. Development and transformation of a partnership or marriage.
SO/PL=MO The glandular action. The circulation. The influence of the daily habits on physical development. The physical development of females (of a female).
MO/PL=SO Function of the glands of the body. General rhythm of the hour, the meal, etc. Disorderly changed course of the day. The passing of a certain hour of the day.
#
SO/MO=CU Legal bonds of marriage. Harmonious and sociable intimate relationships. Leisure hours of the day.
SO/CU=MO The bride and bridegroom. Connections between men and women. The betrothed man before the wedding. A woman's fiance. Societies composed of women. To find oneself frequently in groups, associations or in a community of people.
MO/CU=SO The bride. Man's wife. Family home. Repeating hours of leisure.
#
SO/MO=HA Miserable married life, partnership or friendship. The prostitute. Sad hours. Dealing with disagreeable matters on a daily basis.
SO/HA=MO Mentally sick. Debilitated body of a woman, misshapen. Trouble through dubious female persons. Vulgar common people.
MO/HA=SO The poor wife of the man. Daily repeating worries.
#
SO/MO=ZE Days and hours in which a great deal is accomplished. The daily urge for productive activity.
SO/ZE=MO The husband of a woman (procreation). To back women. Inflammable. Burning body. To be sitting on a powder keg (in a figurative sense). Leader of the people.
MO/ZE=SO To master one's body. A sense for discipline and order. The daily repeated working hours. Wanting to assist. Energetic interference. Easily inflammable substance. The hour of the commencement of a fire.
#
SO/MO=KR The time when great events take place for the native. A marriage before the registrar. To marry for position (or rank). To marry someone of the same social standing. To appear as a leader daily and hourly. To make arrangement.
SO/KR=MO Noble descent. High birth. Wife of a prince. Leader of federations. Noble persons.
MO/KR=SO The independent wife. Daily office and consultation hours.
#
SO/MO=AP To be successful daily and hourly. To enjoy many differing experiences in life. A time for success. Day of glory for a nation, community or the population.
SO/AP=MO Successes in the public or in connection with women. The glory of a nation.
MO/AP=SO The man who strives for freedom of action. The daily one and the same. Daily and hourly encounter many people.
#
SO/MO=AD The end of friendship, partnership or marriage. Days of no accomplishment. Dead, still day.
SO/AD=MO Deficiency in the functioning of the glands. Death of a woman, the wife or the mother.
MO/AD=SO Daily repeating sad hours. To be daily conscious of crowded conditions. The personal attitude toward death. Day and hour of death. Remaining immovable.
#
SO/MO=VU Times when big things happen. Daily and hourly to exercise or experience executive power.
SO/VU=MO Well functioning glands. The physical strength of a woman. To demonstrate or apply physical strength in public. Persons with executive power.
MO/VU=SO The man who causes public interest. The daily repeating forceful events. The daily being together with people of public interest and influence.
#
SO/MO=PO Days and hours devoted to spiritual things. To find partnerships, friendships and marriage partner based on spiritual/mental attraction. Intellectual friendships. Spiritualization.
SO/PO=MO Spiritualized women. Sense for spiritual things.
MO/PO=SO The daily repeating hours of susceptibility. To be in company with such people.
#
SO/KN=ME Thoughts and news concerning a personal union.
SO/ME=KN Union of thought (a mental relationship). Body language. To express a thought physically...to wink, beckon, etc. The actor.
KN/ME=SO Verbal exchange of ideas.
#
SO/KN=VE Physical love union.
SO/VE=KN Flirtations. Love affairs. Celebrating festivities. Attending shows. The Playgoer.
KN/VE=SO Love ties of the body. Agreeable unions.
#
SO/KN=MA A sexual relationship. Activity performed with others.
SO/MA=KN Associations with men, the husband (in a woman's horoscope) or soldiers through work.
KN/MA=SO Connections through the activity of the body. Sex unions of one's own body.
#
SO/KN=JU A fortunate physical union.
SO/JU=KN Wealthy connections. Support from successful people. To prepare the way for happiness and well being.
KN/JU=SO Good bodily connections. Good personal connections.
#
SO/KN=SA The connection with the public is made difficult. It is severed. Physical separations.
SO/SA=KN Unions or relationships with older people. Physical severance of relationships.
KN/SA=SO Separation from the husband or from a man.
#
SO/KN=UR To be involved suddenly in current events. Physical excitement through a relationship.
SO/UR=KN To be present at an accident. An unexpected meeting or connection with a man. To be excited over a personal relationship.
KN/UR=SO To be suddenly embroiled in something.
#
SO/KN=NE The cooling off of a relationship. Damage to the health because of another person. To be susceptible to disease or poisonings. Legerdemain (sleight of hand).
SO/NE=KN inherited blood diseases. Acquaintance with delicate people.
KN/NE=SO Untenable connections or unions. Divorce. To be deserted.
#
SO/KN=PL Relationships formed in relation to diet or nutrition. New connections bring changes.
SO/PL=KN To be able to develop nourishing relationships, physically healthy for the native (providing the Node is in good pictures).
KN/PL=SO Much change in the personal unions and connections. Ties in connection with foodstuff s. To set new connections while buying and selling food.
#
SO/KN=CU A relationship that leads to marriage. Socially involved ties. People in the arts.
SO/CU=KN To proceed to marry. Relationships with artists.
KN/CU=SO Marriages. Forms of society. Associations.
#
SO/KN=HA To be taken ill. Relationships with rabble. Physically disgusting and repulsive connections. To be threatened with imprisonment.
SO/HA=KN Sorrowful, dubious connections. Pursued by misfortune. Acquaintance with criminals.
KN/HA=SO To meet invalids. To be bodily disgusted arid fed up with something. Bodily displeasure. To be robbed. To unite with thieves.
#
SO/KN=ZE Associated with self-sufficient, motivated or military persons. To come into physical contact with fire.
SO/ZE=KN Associated with creative men. Allies in a fight. To see or to experience a fire.
KN/ZE=SO To be a producer or procreator. Coming into personal contact with fire. To be commissioned with or to carry out a special task. To be a member of an outfit that blows things up with dynamite.
#
SO/KN=KR Associated with officials, superiors and capable people.
SO/KR=KN Connections with the authorities or leading persons. Manager. Representative of the government. Embassy.
KN/KR=SO entangled with the officials. To be summoned.
#
SO/KN=AP Close ties to successful people, dignitaries, scientists and executives in commerce. Many opportunities in life to nurture successful personal relations.
SO/AP=KN Many and far-reaching connections, with successes through them.
KN/AP=SO Success due to personal freedom. To evolve unhindered. Connected with business people, scientists or many persons, promotion through these. Free people among each other.
#
SO/KN=AD No ability to make contacts.
SO/AD=KN Definitively separated connections. Connections with estate or landscape. To be bound to the soil.
KN/AD=SO A few people come together for a particular reason. To be captivated in bonds.
#
SO/KN=VU To have mighty and influential connections.
SO/VU=KN Connection with a strong, muscular man. To suffer from other persons. Wish for power.
KN/VU=SO To be eager. To exert great powerful influence by personal appearance or participation.
#
SO/KN=PO Intellectual associations and unions with others.
SO/PO=KN To be connected with spiritual things. Connections with spiritualized man or spirits respectively.
KN/PO=SO Being bodily together with others of the same mind or mentality. Bodily contact with people of high mentality.
#
SO/ME=VE A Young girl. Love of a young body.
SO/VE=ME Verbal expressions of love. Thinking about expressing love. A man discussing love. Love relationships.
ME/VE=SO Personal meeting or greeting of friends or new acquaintances. Love of one's own body. Vanity. Taste for plastic arts.
#
SO/ME=MA A young boy. A young man. To work fast. The tempo of work. Violent thinking. Anger an excitement.
SO/MA=ME The commander. The man who becomes angry and tends toward assault.
ME/MA=SO The voice. The language. Quickly acting body. Excited body.
#
SO/ME=JU A lucky youth (also to be lucky in youth). A good natured and benign mind. One who is agile and serene. The talent for speaking. Travelling for pleasure.
SO/JU=ME A good physician. Good news. Thinking of success. Persevering speaker. Success through speaking, travel, writing, commerce or trade professions.
ME/JU=SO Happy mood. Lively and agile body.
#
SO/ME=SA The state of consciousness is retarded. The faculty to became aware of something is slowed down. To be engaged in serious practical thinking. To take leave (time of prating). Travelling. The end of a journey (the arrival).
SO/SA=ME Parting. To go away. To travel. To occupy one's mind with personal restraints. To talk and write about restrains. Thinking about separating.
ME/SA=SO Parting person. Unruly guest. Not staying long in one place. To roam.
#
SO/ME=UR To think by leaps and bounds. To talk fast. The inventor or technician. Sudden quick thoughts. To demonstrate or give demonstrations. An uproar involving young people. To suddenly act on a matter.
SO/UR=ME To comprehend fast. A day filled with news, calls messages. To have spontaneous ideas. Quick and vivid thinking. To give orders suddenly. To be excited while speaking or writing. A rescue team present at an accident.
ME/UR=SO Agility. Quick acting body. Quick at hand. Messenger.
#
SO/ME=NE Inspiration. The author. Not clear, rambling, impractical thinking. To be sluggish. A swindler. Fraud.
SO/NE=ME Poor memory. Day-dreaming. A receptive mind. Not being fully awake. Mental defects. The feeling of mental ecstasy. To be confused about the personal sphere of life.
ME/NE=SO Receptivity for thoughts, author.
#
SO/ME=PL Thinking that is capable of development. Changeable thinking (flexible). The influence of the external motion on the internal development, the growth an the circulation.
SO/PL=ME The exercising body. To be in motion. The physical development speeds up through exercise. Increased agility of the body through exercise. The condition of the nerves.
ME/PL=SO Function of the motoric and vegetative nervous system. To prefer nerve-strengthening food.
#
SO/ME=CU An early marriage. Thoughts about community, marriage an art. Male relatives (young). One's in-laws.
SO/CU=ME Thinking of marriage. News of a marriage. Artistic thinking. Honeymoon trips. A person with an exclusive interest in local club life.
ME/CU=SO Professional artist. Physical beauty.
#
SO/ME=HA Sickness in youth. Children's diseases. Dirty thoughts and actions.
SO/HA=ME To think of sick and unhappy people. Diseases of the head or face. To be familiar with dirty matters. Opinions meeting with rejection and resistance. To appear repulsive. Messenger of bad news. To threaten. To instill fear.
ME/HA=SO To be sick of nerves. A body with a weak nervous system. A young rowdy. To be the victim of sarcasm.
#
SO/ME=ZE To be mentally methodical, purposeful and commanding. To make a proposal. To wish to put something into action.
SO/ZE=ME The chief of the press. To be creative through writing and talking. An announcement of a fire. A Fire-alarm. To order or lead an attack.
ME/ZE=SO Decrees of the day. The justice (judge). The superior in person. To be condemned.
#
SO/ME=KR Mental accomplishments during youth. Boldly conceived and honorable thinking. Setting the fashion. Commands that must be carried out. Decrees. Prescriptions. Orders.
SO/KR=ME independent thinking. To be fit for intellectual leadership. Chief.
ME/KR=SO To conceive great thoughts. A body ennobled through the mind (Spirit). Father's word. Government head.
#
SO/ME=AP Success and experiences in the time of youth. Broad encompassing thinking. Far-reaching thoughts. To speak several languages. To talk much.
SO/AP=ME To talk about one's successes. Linguistic talent. Faculty of speech. Vainglorious. Successful orator or author. To brag.
ME/AP=SO Much exercise. Scientific success. The day of glory for a scientist.
#
SO/ME=AD To change from motion into a state of rest. The resting body. To die in youth. Consciousness focused on specific things.
SO/AD=ME Depressed thinking. The mind and thoughts are busy with the soil.
ME/AD=SO Little exercise. To end a motion. The body at a standstill. No bodily perseverance. To be driven in a vehicle. Sedentary mode of life. The office-staff.
#
SO/ME=VU A wide-ranging consciousness. impressive speaking and thinking capacity. Great agility of body. The momentum of a body in action. Speedy action.
SO/VU=ME To have strong nerves. Emphatic words. A runner.
ME/VU=SO To be physically and routinely in motion. A life or mode of living which demands much moving around. Great speed of a body.
#
SO/ME=PO Spiritual and mental matters in youth. To concentrate on ideas and writings. Spiritualized thinking and writing.
SO/PO=ME The mental agility. To perceive new ideas.
ME/PO=SO Spiritual conception of being. The spiritualized body.
#
SO/VE=MA A man's love activity.

#
SO/VE=UR To be sentimental. To have a refined feeling of love. One who is full of expression. A very fine perception of love. Love at first sight. A man who is suddenly in love. To be very affectionate. Refined sensuality. Artistic and original art.

VE/UR=SO Delicate feeling. To be loved suddenly. High minded love. Courtesy.
#
SO/VE=NE Overly sensitive in love-affairs. Love without prospects. Unhappy love of a man. Coolness in expressing affection.
SO/NE=VE Shyness. The chaste lover.
VE/NE=SO Timid in love. Silent admirer. Indifferent in love.

SO/PL=VE To develop the taste. The body grows and develops harmoniously (in balance and proportion).
VE/PL=SO Harmonious development of the body. Harmonious passing of the days. Pleasant state of nourishment of the body. To eat in pleasant surroundings.
#
SO/VE=CU A man in love. One who is inclined to marry. The formula for a man on his wedding day. A beautiful, artfully shaped body.
SO/CU=VE A love match. An affectionate community of people. A community or marriage in harmony.
VE/CU=SO Marital happiness of the man. To be in pleasant company.

JU/PL=SO Fortunate run of the day. Happy, fortunate physical development. The man who is accustomed to take abundant meals.
