KN/MA=HA Unpleasantness during working hours. Free purchasable love. To join together for evil activities.
KN/HA=MA To call together or be together for the purpose of committing mean acts.
MA/HA=KN Connections which make success difficult or impossible. To be connected with murder or ugly affairs.
#
KN/MA=ZE Efforts of a work-community. Accomplishments of a creative nature. Sex union with consequences.
KN/ZE=MA To incite to action. To perform something together. To play tricks. To be singled out or chosen for a work. To have to do something.
MA/ZE=KN To unite oneself with producers. Rifle club.
#
KN/MA=KR Meeting for great activities. Blood-relatives of the father.
KN/KR=MA To contact the authorities through one's own activities. To be taken into custody. To be arrested.
MA/KR=KN Connected with independently acting persons. To get into touch with authorities. To use government help.
#
KN/MA=AP Scientific work project. Working together with many, or in industry and commerce.
KN/AP=MA Free commercial activity. Far reaching connections through scientific activities or business matters or with representatives of commerce and science.
MA/AP=KN Connections with managers, business people or dealers.
#
KN/MA=AD Working community in a small circle. To be a participant in or witness to terrorist acts. Clique.
KN/AD=MA To work together with few. Small working community. Working alone. To reject a working community or to be rejected by a joint-stock company. Connected by fighting for freedom.
MA/AD=KN Connected with strongly depressed or pessimistic persons.
#
KN/MA=VU Mighty work-community or common activity.
KN/VU=MA To be busy. Very industrious. To display eager action.
MA/VU=KN Connected with physically strong and energetic people.
#
KN/MA=PO Common activity with others on account of similar moral or ideological inclinations.
KN/PO=MA Connections gained through one's own mental accomplishments.
MA/PO=KN Connections through ethics, morals and intellectual activity.
#
KN/JU=SA Dissolution of a betrothal or marriage or good connection.
KN/SA=JU Quick and pleasant separations. The separation of a happy union or satisfaction through such.
JU/SA=KN Separation of a happy union, or happy separation of a union. Co-occupants.
#
KN/JU=UR Unexpected good acquaintance.
KN/UR=JU To enjoy advantages. To come into money suddenly through acquaintances.
JU/UR=KN Sudden fortunate union.
#
KN/JU=NE Gradual cooling off of friendship.
KN/NE=JU To suffer money losses through common interests. False speculations.
JU/NE=KN Lucky feeling through connections; Pretense about lucky connections. Participants in speculation.
#
KN/JU=PL Getting acquainted for a fortunate connection.
KN/PL=JU Fortunate changes in the connections.
JU/PL=KN Fortunate change in connections. New good connections.
#
KN/JU=CU Good connection leading to marriage.
KN/CU=JU Jolly wedding. Hilarity in social company. Fortunate social connection.
JU/CU=KN To close an acquaintance that leads to happy marriage. Connections with good corporations.
#
KN/JU=HA Good relations to single women or persons of socially lower levels.
KN/HA=JU To be prompted or to prompt others to misappropriate or to extort something. Unpleasantness in money affairs. To be a witness to thefts.
JU/HA=KN To be entangled in money difficulties. Connected with people who have hard luck.
#
KN/JU=ZE Increase in the family in prospect. Fortunate common creation.
KN/ZE=JU Favorable choice. Joyful co-operation, Fortunate working together. Working with jewelry.
JU/ZE=KN Connected with work and with production. To undertake something with others.
#
KN/JU=KR Good relations of the father or of the chief. Protection. Acquaintance with the future husband.
KN/KR=JU Court action. Good connections with authorities. Reward. To receive money.
JU/KR=KN Connection with leading persons or with people who experience great luck.
#
KN/JU=AP To be happily joined with many. Fine connections with merchants, scientists.
KN/AP=JU Luck of freedom. Fortunately united with many economically or scientifically.
JU/AP=KN Happily connected with many, Connections through science and commerce.
#
KN/JU=AD The end of a happy union.
KN/AD=JU To be modest. To be happy and fortunate with few. Content with little. To have a modest stroke of fortune. Free from bonds.
JU/AD=KN Feeling lucky through connection with few.
#
KN/JU=VU influence and might through fortunate connections.
KN/VU=JU Happily tied to influential persons.
JU/VU=KN To be connected with trustworthy people.
#
KN/JU=PO Fortunate spiritual relations. Fortunate connection with prudent and with people.
KN/PO=JU Aligned with reasonable and prudent people. Connected with wise and philosophically educated people.
JU/PO=KN Connections with intelligent and reasonable people. Happiness through intellectual and spiritual connections.
#
KN/SA=UR Sudden separations from friends or relatives.
KN/UR=SA Witness of a scene or of somebody turned out. To participate suddenly in separations.
SA/UR=KN To be present at separations. Connections torn apart.
#
KN/SA=NE To be separated through sickness or falsehood.
KN/NE=SA Losses because of separations. Tied for good to something very disagreeable.
SA/NE=KN Connections with diseased people, with sick people.
#
KN/SA=PL Divorce which requires a long time Slow cooling off of connections or unions.
KN/PL=SA New connections by separating from the old ones. New connections which become effective in old age, but not at present. Change of connections at a later age.
SA/PL=KN To be tied to people, things or conditions, to which one gets very slowly used to. Separated connections.
#
KN/SA=CU Separation of a marriage or union.
KN/CU=SA Dissolution of a betrothal. Breach of promise to marry. Loss in the family. Separation of a familiar or social connection.
SA/CU=KN To separate from the parents, brothers or sisters.
#
KN/SA=HA To be separated by sickness, To retire. Separation through enmity or unpleasant fatal blow.
KN/HA=SA To be tied to hostile people. Loss by theft or because of poor company.
SA/HA=KN To be tied up to loathsome, distasteful things. To come into contact with seriously ill persons or corpses.
#
KN/SA=ZE To take leave from the parents or from the husband. Forced separation.
KN/ZE=SA To drop a matter.
SA/ZE=KN Work agreements where everybody works for himself. To be co-sufferer in fire losses.
#
KN/SA=KR Disagreeableness through the chief. Restriction through the State. To be separated by the government or some superior power.
KN/KR=SA Loss of position. Discharge from custody. Hindrances by the power of the state. To be under arrest. To receive notice.
SA/KR=KN Connection with persons or corporations of economical instability (Mergers of weak corporations).
#
KN/SA=AP Many are inhibiting. Breaking off of connections with many. Many part with something.
KN/AP=SA Limited freedom. Connected with many through methods and systems. Hindrances and separations in economic and scientific connections.
SA/AP=KN To give up the connections with many, to retire. Many have to interrupt their connections.
#
KN/SA=AD Enduring and close connections, Connections with a few.
KN/AD=SA End of an oppression. Separating connection with a few. Oppressive bonds. To pant under a heavy burden.
SA/AD=KN Definitely broken off connections. To be without connections. To be alone.
#
KN/SA=VU Wanting for an event. To feel depressed through separation from a union. To be under supervision (control) or to supervise something.
KN/VU=SA To be forced. To submit to force. Separation of a stable connection.
SA/VU=KN To experience strong hindrances in or through one's connections. To be connected with people who themselves are attentive and vigilant.
#
KN/SA=PO Separation of unions or connections through mental differences. To deal with educational matters. Through such matters connected with others.
KN/PO=SA To have to deal with educators and pupils. Serious or separated mental connections.
SA/PO=KN Connections with educators. Separation of connections caused by differences in outlook on life.
#
KN/UR=NE To become a widower or widow suddenly. To be suddenly connected with a disagreeable heritage. Taking over things or matters highly disorganized. To be connected with chaotic things.
KN/NE=UR Sudden connections which are not tenable. Passing union or connections.
UR/NE=KN Connections in the state of dissolution. Connections with dead people. To die.
#
KN/UR=PL Suddenly arising new connections.
KN/PL=UR Surprisingly sudden new connections.
UR/PL=KN Connected with sudden changes.
#
KN/UR=CU Connection to a quick marriage. Surprisingly connected with one's own or another family. To have family visitors, or to be a visitor oneself.
KN/CU=UR Sudden events of a betrothed couple or in the family. Commotion or excitement in an association or firm.
UR/CU=KN Connection leading to quick marriage. To decide quickly upon a union.
#
KN/UR=HA To have trouble with unpleasant matters suddenly. To be entangled in murder affairs.
KN/HA=UR Suddenly connected with evil things. To be present at accidents or murder.
UR/HA=KN To be suddenly connected with ugly and cunning treachery or a surprisingly setting in need and distress. Connections with murder affairs.
#
KN/UR=ZE To be forced into a union, or to get your way by force. To be visited by fire or explosion. To expect progeny.
KN/ZE=UR To experience, in company with others, a sudden and unexpected event. To be in a fire ... in the flames.
UR/ZE=KN To join for energetic work. In connection with the forces of nature. Major forces.
#
KN/UR=KR Sudden connections with the power of the state.
KN/KR=UR Sudden connections with the authorities.
UR/KR=KN Intervention of the ruling power.
#
KN/UR=AP Sudden business connections or with scientists.
KN/AP=UR Sudden expansion of the freedom or of the sphere of activity. Connected suddenly with scientists and economists. Connections through scientific predictions.
UR/AP=KN To be connected with people who are under tense excitement and expectation. Sudden expansion of connections.
#
KN/UR=AD Sudden breaking off of connections.
KN/AD=UR Sudden active excitement with few. To break their chains. Sudden break in connection with a few.
UR/AD=KN Surprising break-off of connections.
#
KN/UR=VU Suddenly connected with mighty influential people and with superiors. Examiners.
KN/VU=UR To be suddenly, severely checked over. Sudden powerful union. To show great zeal quite suddenly.
UR/VU=KN To be investigated, examined, checked or rechecked. To be reckless and inconsiderate. To be carried along. To be dragged along. To be suddenly connected with great energies.
#
KN/UR=PO Connections through common mental efforts. Connections through common search for truth and understanding. Wanting to clear up something together with others.
KN/PO=UR Mentally connected with others in a surprising manner. Sudden clarity in connection with or in relation to others.
UR/PO=KN To be connected with representatives of an idea. To have dealings with propagandists.
#
KN/NE=PL Unnoticeable unions. Connections made without the native's knowledge behind his back, but discovered after.
KN/PL=NE Connections of a very passing nature.
NE/PL=KN Connections which slowly come and go, hardly noticed.
#
KN/NE=CU Separation of a marital union. Dissolution of the union with one's own family.
KN/CU=NE Postponed engagement. Liquidated corporation. Tie-ups or to join with a secret society or organization.
NE/CU=KN To break off connections. To make connections which turn out to be unsatisfactory.
#
KN/NE=HA To find oneself in a hopeless sorrowful condition. Not knowing a way out. To be separated from, or shunned by others on account of poverty.
KN/HA=NE Passing connection with evil things. To have to do with water. To experience water accidents. Danger of drowning.
NE/HA=KN To experience disappointments in connection with others. To be deceived about one's connections. To deal with liquids. Cloudbursts. To experience water catastrophes.
#
KN/NE=ZE The end of a creative period. The end of a fight. Settling an argument.
KN/ZE=NE Useless effort of several people.
NE/ZE=KN Spontaneous union with consequent separation.
#
KN/NE=KR Bad contact with the state. Through power of the state dissolved unions. The deserted leader.
KN/KR=NE Respite. Release from prison. To be divorced. Connections among the leading people dissolve. Unsuccessful connection with the authorities.
NE/KR=KN Secret doings. Compelled to have dealings with insincere people. To experience maneuvers of deception. Connection with inefficient management or managers.
#
KN/NE=AP Dissolution of union with many.
KN/AP=NE Abuse of freedom. Not to know what to do with freedom. Dissolution of connection with many, but also connections with many through science of future.
NE/AP=KN To be connected with people who distort the truth, who are insincere.
#
KN/NE=AD Connections or ties that cool off completely. Slow but definite breaking up of connections or unions.
KN/AD=NE Dissolving connection with few. To free oneself from bonds, to untie.
NE/AD=KN To be part or to suffer through changes in new conditions of living.
#
KN/NE=VU To experience mighty disappointments through present connections. Dissolution of present connections with grave consequences.
KN/VU=NE Difficult to be caught through application of force. Connected with confused power. Tainted mighty connections. Dissolution of mighty connections.
NE/VU=KN Maneuvers of deception. To deceive others successfully.
#
KN/NE=PO High minded efforts with others. Dissolution of spiritual connections. To be forced to have dealings with Fantastic and unreliable people.
KN/PO=NE Relations to high minded people. Dissolution of present relations.
NE/PO=KN To have dealings with desperate people, to be connected with highly developed or occult people. To be fooled by erroneous occult teachings.
#
KN/PL=CU Changes in the family, of the members of the family, in number. Social changes. Old connections are taken up again.
KN/CU=PL Previous connections are started all over. Newly arising community. Assembly of a corporation. Inauguration (opening) of parliament.
PL/CU=KN To be connected with changed conditions of being.
#
KN/PL=HA New connections, which prove to be an absolute failure, untenable.
KN/HA=PL Promises that are not kept. Connections that fail.
PL/HA=KN Connection with persons who disappoint, who tall short of expectations. The same with things. To be connected with untenable things, eager for or thinking about remedial measures.
#
KN/PL=ZE To conclude new connections for the increase of one's accomplishment. One's increased accomplishments bring new connections.
KN/ZE=PL Several people increase their accomplishments. To quarrel about superiority.
PL/ZE=KN To begin ... to start work and to tackle a problem.
#
KN/PL=KR Connections in which one is the pupil, the beginner.
KN/KR=PL Desire to dominate. Developments, changes in connection with the authorities.
PL/KR=KN To be affected by great changes or respectively, to experience changes in one's connections.
#
KN/PL=AP New connections with many, with scientists, commerce, trade or industry.
KN/AP=PL Increasing desire for liberty. Increase of authority. Increasing scientific and economic connections. Great volume of new connections of this kind.
PL/AP=KN To create new test cases.
#
KN/PL=AD New connections which are sound and of long duration.
KN/AD=PL Fundamental transformation in connection with a few. Weakening or strengthening the bonds.
PL/AD=KN A radical change in one's connections. To make new endurable connections.
#
KN/PL=VU New connections of great importance for the native.
KN/VU=PL To develop eagerness. Forced changes.
PL/VU=KN To get into a whirlpool, to be overpowered by a mighty transformation.
#
KN/PL=PO New connections which are of importance for the mental and spiritual development.
KN/PO=PL Transformations and changes in mental relations.
PL/PO=KN The change of mind or heart of others forces the native to change his own mind or heart. Also the reverse may be the case. Mental development furthered through connections.
#
KN/CU=HA Death in the family. Funeral rites. A weakly founded poor firm.
KN/HA=CU Poverty, lack or other circumstances strain family relations. To experience something evil in company with others. Sociability in criminal circles.
CU/HA=KN Connection with firms of insufficient economic basis. To be concerned in bankruptcies. To be a witness to an unfortunate or sad event.
#
KN/CU=ZE Wanting union and combination. To tend toward association. Creative work together with others.
KN/ZE=CU To found associations (corporations). To be forced into a union.
CU/ZE=KN Co-founder of a corporation.
#
KN/CU=KR To go to the marriage official, Distinguished social connection. To mix socially with high minded persons or superiors.
KN/KR=CU Civil marriages. Social connections with superiors or official persons.
CU/KR=KN To wish to establish a family. Legal witness of a marriage. Connection with great art.
#
KN/CU=AP Many social connections with scientists, commercial people, industry and craftsmen.
KN/AP=CU Free or many social connections. Commercial or scientific connections with many.
CU/AP=KN In connection with art and science.
#
KN/CU=AD Rejected or refused by one's own relatives. A small closed community. Few or no social connections.
KN/AD=CU Few members of family. Reduction of family. To retain only a few social connections. Loneliness in connection with a few. Heavily oppressed communities.
CU/AD=KN In connection with people who are lonely and avoided. A small or limited union with shunned or avoided persons. To be a witness of a breaking up or dissolution of a partnership or community, to partake. Clique.
#
KN/CU=VU Family convention. Mighty influential social connections. Social unions or ties to reinforce common power and influence.
KN/VU=CU Deeds of violence through a community. Mighty influence of a community. Mighty connections through influential community.
CU/VU=KN To be connected with a mighty society, family or great art.
#
KN/CU=PO In mental and spiritual union joined with others. To come into contact with religious or similar sects.
KN/PO=CU Connections through mental associations, sects etc. Member of such unions.
CU/PO=KN To deal with matters of faith. In connection with confessions, sects or churches.
#
KN/HA=ZE To be criticized for short comings. To come into touch with the police on account of crime. To be involved by fire.
KN/ZE=HA Instigation of trouble by several people. To be impoverished by fire.
HA/ZE=KN To participate in large damage. Connection with the industry or industrial class (industrial workers). Connection with strikes.
#
KN/HA=KR To make common cause with criminals or to avail oneself of criminal assistance. To partake in a conspiracy or plot.
KN/KR=HA Deplorable conditions in leading circles. Corruption. Sorrow or loss through connection with authorities. Connection with authorities on account of shameful actions, crimes.
HA/KR=KN To be witness to a great crime or great accident. To be a party to doubtful things. To be active as a spy. To be hired. Connection with the criminal investigation department.
#
KN/HA=AP To be connected with many through evil things. Many vexations, disagreeableness. Science Historical Society .
KN/AP=HA Poor but free. Many people in this situation.
HA/AP=KN Stoppage of trade. Decline of economics. In connection with science of antiquity or science of history. Accomplice to hidden things.
#
KN/HA=AD Disagreeableness in a small circle. Junk dealer.
KN/AD=HA To suffer a lack of life's necessities with a few. Lack, poverty, sickness, disagreeableness hinder.
HA/AD=KN To come in touch with poverty. Connections which are depressive.
#
KN/HA=VU Through ugly things tied together. Dangerous gang of criminals.
KN/VU=HA Vile acts by mighty persons.
HA/VU=KN To face a danger directly. To have dealings with coarse and uncouth people. To be harassed by them.
#
KN/HA=PO Wisdom of the antiquity. Secret or occult society. To be connected through forbidden ideological conceptions.
KN/PO=HA To be tied to others whose mentality is far below that of the native.
HA/PO=KN To be associated with odd people.
#
KN/ZE=KR To meet in order to produce something great. The machine-master.
KN/KR=ZE Decision of authorities. The verdict. To be sentenced.
ZE/KR=KN To join with others for great deeds. War comrades. Allied nations in war.
#
KN/ZE=AP To work together with many. To carry arms or to know how to handle them.
KN/AP=ZE Creation through freedom. Combined activity in domestic economy and science.
ZE/AP=KN To be jointly trained with arms, machines, apparatus and other technical implements.
#
KN/ZE=AD A work-community fighting against odds, great obstacles and opposition. To have to overcome all obstacles at the beginning.
KN/AD=ZE Start work with a few. Difficult beginning of work with others.
ZE/AD=KN To be called upon to solve new problems. To be part of solving problems. To be involved in changed conditions. To be, in part, the cause of changed conditions.
#
KN/ZE=VU Several make a mighty effort. Great efforts together with others.
KN/VU=ZE Leading in a mighty connection. To be under the pressure or compulsion of a powerful organization. Threats.
ZE/VU=KN To be exposed to the effects of great strength or power. To be connected with military things, machines, arms, or other implements.
#
KN/ZE=PO Spiritual creations in common with others.
KN/PO=ZE To hold mental leadership. To be outstanding in mental connections.
ZE/PO=KN To have to issue a document.
#
KN/KR=AP Connections with scientific celebrities. Connected, along with many other people, with the authorities.
KN/AP=KR Great and free or proud and free. Connected with authorities, courts, officials.
KR/AP=KN To have or to make connections with scientists, Extremely far expanded limits.
#
KN/KR=AD Official meeting with a few. Close connections with the chief or the authorities. Latent connections with authorities. Connection with dethroned persons.
KN/AD=KR Bad contact with the authorities or the chief. The connection with leading persons, with superiors, with authorities is interrupted. Dealing with " has-beens." Government without power.
KR/AD=KN Connected with persons who lead an odd life. To be connected with odd characters.
#
KN/KR=VU Mighty influential connections. Connection with power of the state.
KN/VU=KR To be subjected to power by the state. Connected with armed forces of state.
KR/VU=KN In connection with the power of the State, with the forces of the state.
#
KN/KR=PO Connection through great spiritual accomplishment. Connections with intellectually leading men.
KN/PO=KR Connections with mentally, superior people. To let others influence oneself.
KR/PO=KN To meet for mental endeavor. Cultural associations. Connections born out of the same mental attitude.
#
KN/AP=AD From freedom under coercion. Small economic or scientific communities. To be content with freedom or coercion.
KN/AD=AP Contentment with a few. From bonds to freedom. Satisfied and contented with one's own connections.
AP/AD=KN To be satisfied with the existing circumstances. To have the necessary connections.
#
KN/AP=VU Unlimited freedom. Expansive power. Strong and outstanding successes in connection with science and industry.
KN/VU=AP Success and promotion through mighty connection. To cause much excitement, excitation. Many get excited.
AP/VU=KN A�antages through the relationships that are formed. To be right and well connected.
#
KN/AP=PO The feeling of freedom among one's equals. To be connected with many through similar world concept or same religion.
KN/PO=AP To be mentally connected with many. Connections which occur on account of similar views in relation to world affairs or science.
AP/PO=KN To be connected with comrades, colleagues, fellow sufferers or congenial persons.
#
KN/AD=VU To display extraordinary activities with few. Undertaking great things with few. To be heavily chained or to burst the chains.
KN/VU=AD Mighty connection with few. End of a mighty union or connection.
AD/VU=KN Connected with things in which one is severely handicapped. To be under the impact of a heavy blow of fate.
#
KN/AD=PO To be in mental or spiritual harmony with a few. Cultural work in a small circle. During education to be subjected to severe restrictions and limitations.
KN/PO=AD In mental or spiritual community with a few To be connected with cultured people.
AD/PO=KN To be connected with educational courses, educated people, educational establishments etc.
#
KN/VU=PO Eagerness mentally as well as spiritually. Mighty connection through mental faculties.
KN/PO=VU To be noticed in mental connections through eagerness and alertness. influence in or through mental connections.
VU/PO=KN To be connected with people who have pride and dignity. Relationships with the type of people where one can count on honesty and decency. Powerful intellectual associations. To be eager to cultivate the finer intellectual contacts.
#
ME/VE=MA Acquaintances which become intimate. Passionate. Acquaintances in military circles. Military greeting. Fond of physical activities. Wrought-iron worker.
ME/MA=VE The pleasant voice. Harmonious debates with good friends. Discussions about sex matters. Lover's quarrels.
VE/MA=ME Erotic. Sexual thoughts. Younger relatives or intimate acquaintances. To think or act with devotion.
#
ME/VE=JU Happy new acquaintance. Good manners. Sense of humor. Friendly. Fond of ornaments. Jeweler.
ME/JU=VE Lover's discourse. Teasing. Pleasant conversation. Agreements in ideas.
VE/JU=ME Thoughts of happy love. Joy through confiding in friends.
#
ME/VE=SA Acquaintance with older or more serious people. Travelling-acquaintance. Falling out with friends, quarrelling with them, to separate. Retiring disposition. To be surly. Wood carver.
ME/SA=VE New friends one separates from. Separations from or through acquaintances. To become unfaithful. To propose divorce.
VE/SA=ME To think of hindrances in love. Thoughts of parting (divorce). Separation from acquaintances.
#
ME/VE=UR Sudden acquaintances, quick at repartee. Witty, original. Strange forms of social intercourse. Mathematical sense.
ME/UR=VE Sudden love. Sense for rhythmic or plastic beauty. Singing. Sudden pleasant news. Sudden news from friends. Excitement with or through acquaintances.
VE/UR=ME To think of love spontaneously. Erotic thoughts. To make surprisingly pleasant new connections. Art impulses.
#
ME/VE=NE Untenable friends or acquaintances. Not to recognize acquaintances. Careless in one's own behavior. Infidelity. Poetic, story teller, fantastic person.
ME/NE=VE Superficial acquaintances. Love dream. Love poem. To adore. Adored through acquaintances.
VE/NE=ME Much imagination in love. Full of enthusiasm. Acquaintance with persons full of enthusiasm.
#
ME/VE=PL Deep reaching change in acquaintances. Development of beauty, art, love. Development of greeting or personal behavior.
ME/PL=VE To desire people, to wish for people in whom one can trust, and get along with harmoniously. New harmonious acquaintances. New love acquaintances.
VE/PL=ME To pave the way for a pleasant change. New acquaintances prepare the way for a pleasant change. To realize the pleasant change of things.
#
ME/VE=CU New acquaintances through social, family relations or through art work. Social manners. Master of ceremonies. Solo performer of a musical instrument (violin etc.).
ME/CU=VE Acquaintances through speeches, Language, music, social conversation or the family. Art craft. To make ornaments. Pieces of jewelry.
VE/CU=ME To meet the family of the bride. Thoughts about happiness in married life. Acquaintances through the family.
#
ME/VE=HA Acquaintance with vulgar things or base men. Bad manners. Rudeness. Unethical artistic occupation. Immoral performances. Preference for riches of the earth.
ME/HA=VE Sad thoughts of love. Weakness of the sexual nerves. Acquaintances who cause slander and bad reputation. Art in the dives. Dirty jokes.
VE/HA=ME To have one's thoughts concentrated upon the service one is doing. Thinking about service relations. Obscene Jokes. Owner of a brothel. Mediator, agent.
#
ME/VE=ZE Acquaintances by order or work. To be forced to greet the superiors. Incendiary mania (Pyromania).. Pyrotechnics. Artist with fireworks.
ME/ZE=VE Well meaning arrangements, dispositions or orders. Decrees about moral matters and customs. Rules about greeting.
VE/ZE=ME Acquaintances through working together. Thoughts of generation.
#
ME/VE=KR High social connections. Promotion through acquaintance. Polite. Artistic taste of highest degree. Distinguished forms of behavior. Art critic.
ME/KR=VE Acquaintance through great thoughts or ideas. Soft artistic way of expression. Pleasant acquaintanceship with the power of the state. To enjoy advantages through the knowledge of people who know. Artistic form of literature.
VE/KR=ME Charitable thoughts. To come to charitable acquaintances through thinking.
#
ME/VE=AP Many new relations, very popular, sure and good manners.
ME/AP=VE Friendly talks with many acquaintances. Inclination to study languages, scientific debates and science.
VE/AP=ME To be known to many, to speak to many, to be acquainted with many. Acquaintances with scientists of languages or people who speak foreign languages.
#
ME/VE=AD To have few acquaintances. Cold behavior towards new friends. New friends given the cold shoulder. Disorderly conduct.
ME/AD=VE Ungainly in manners. Wrong behavior.
VE/AD=ME Very concentrated thinking. Meditative speculation upon a thought. Aversion against new acquaintance. Aggravation at making new acquaintances.
#
ME/VE=VU New fast friends, well received. Favored by friends. Polite greeting.
ME/VU=VE New acquaintances through powerful thoughts. New acquaintances who become important. To think intensively of new acquaintances.
VE/VU=ME Mighty longing for exchange of thoughts with friends and acquaintances.
#
ME/VE=PO New friends through spiritual matters, through religion, ideas, especially religious ones. Religious rites.
ME/PO=VE To be in touch with ministers, preachers etc.
VE/PO=ME Acquaintanceship with faithful, religious people. Talks about religion and world concept.
#
ME/MA=JU Successful thinking. Merry conversation. Laughing. Valuable debates. Cheerfully proceeding to action.
ME/JU=MA Activity accompanied by merry chatter.
MA/JU=ME Thoughts of betrothal. The will to create something. Successful talks and speeches. Successful orator.
#
ME/MA=SA Serious discussion. Depressed conversation. Quarrel resulting in separation. Death by assassination. Contemplated murder.
ME/SA=MA To be separated through quarrel or dissension. A trip that has to be made.
MA/SA=ME Thoughts or news about sickness, separation and death. Sick-calls. Violent words bring sorrow.
#
ME/MA=UR To talk loud and quickly. inclined to sudden anger. Sudden executed act. Raving.
ME/UR=MA To incite to violence. Suddenly executed action.

JU/KR=PL A great fortune is on the way. Practice makes perfect.
PL/KR=JU The beginning of a fortunate development. Favors through the boss or superiors.
#
JU/PL=AP Agreeable change for many.
JU/AP=PL Great money transaction. Great amounts of money are changing hands, are in circulation.
PL/AP=JU An event with pleasant consequences. A gain happening but once.
#
JU/PL=AD To remain calm in spite of lucky changes, to ignore them, to deny them.
JU/AD=PL A deep fortunate change commences.
PL/AD=JU The fortunate change of destiny. Caution: Make sure that the radical PL is not found in negative pictures.
#
JU/PL=VU Fortunate changes and transformations starting with great force.
JU/VU=PL Great luck begins to develop.
PL/VU=JU To experience a great pleasant change.
#
JU/PL=PO Happy spiritual development.
JU/PO=PL Happy development or reason, intelligence, wisdom and conscience.
PL/PO=JU Reason an good judgement lead to a very fortunate transformation in the religious attitude as well as in the attitude towards the world.
#
JU/CU=HA Happy married life or partnership in modest circumstances or with sad experiences.
JU/HA=CU Poor married life. Money losses of a corporation. Poorly paid art.
CU/HA=JU Decreasing substance. To be and feel happy in sad surroundings. Money through popular or vulgar art. Windfalls in miss-mated marriage.
#

JU/ZE=CU Successful activities of a community. Lucky establishment of a firm. The fortunate beginning in a new firm. Artistic activity.
CU/ZE=JU Fortunate artistic creation. The forming of banks and other financial institutions.

CU/KR=JU Enjoyable leadership and good times concerning a large community. Marriage license. To enjoy the company of cultured people. The signing of a document. Financiers. National banks. Directory of the mint.

JU/AP=CU Enjoying arts with many. Theatre or concerts (visits.) Bank-accounts.
CU/AP=JU The fortune and the happiness of peace. To be joined with many in a fortunate community or association. Fortune through art, science or commerce.
#
JU/CU=AD Small happy group. End of a happy marriage, partnership or art work.
JU/AD=CU Small closed community, small art circle. Art understood only by a few.
CU/AD=JU A small, true community. Passing pleasant company or comradeship.
#
JU/CU=VU Power and influence in or on i community through artistic abilities.
JU/VU=CU Great happiness and joy through art, family or community.
CU/VU=JU Mighty luck or fortune of the family. Great artistic successes. Happiness through enjoying art. Great financial power. High finance.
#

CU/PO=JU To feel happy in a religious or philosophical community. Luck and success with or through such associations.

JU/HA=ZE Innovations not realized because of a shortage of money. Regenerative ability weakened through sickness.
JU/ZE=HA Children without vitality. Agricultural produce. Luck in connection with fuel.

HA/KR=JU To escape mean plots. To get away with a black eye. Finance scandals. Collapse of banks. Inflation.
#
JU/HA=AP Many people are unfortunate, out of luck. Science hindered through lack.

#
JU/HA=VU Not solvent. Crimes caused by lack of money.
JU/VU=HA To escape treachery, or to be successful with treacherous deeds.
HA/VU=JU To be happy despite a state of dependence. To find the necessary protection to avert danger. To escape evil attempts.

HA/PO=JU Happiness through mental isolation. Losses of money through no understanding.

#
JU/ZE=AD A fortune promising, but difficult beginning.

JU/VU=ZE Great happiness through successful creation.
ZE/VU=JU Mighty successful efforts. Important increase of fortune through luck or the own efforts.

ZE/PO=JU The successful proof. To prove the correctness of something. Joy and happiness over the correctness of one's own opinion, conviction, reason and wisdom.

KR/AP=JU Success. Joy, happiness or satisfaction through a leading position or success in science or commerce.

KR/AD=JU To be or to feel happy in a secluded or special position.

KR/VU=JU Successful politics. Increase in territory and growth of the power of the state. Allowances through the power of the state. The successful power of the state.

AP/AD=JU To feel happy and content in one's habits.
#
JU/AP=VU Great increase of property. Mighty income.

AP/VU=JU Great fortune, prosperity, wealth and success.
#

JU/VU=AD To be left in peace. Not to be troubled by anybody. To be happy with a few.
AD/VU=JU To escape, great blows of fate.
#

JU/PO=AD Education, profound insight and wisdom.
AD/PO=JU Joy, happiness and success through education or training.

UR/NE=SA To be thrown into debt or to suffer losses through the deaths of others. Separations through heritage, or dead persons.

UR/PL=SA Abrupt disruption of hitherto development.

SA/UR=CU Quick termination of matrimony. Suspension of clubs and associations.

UR/CU=SA Suddenly separated marriage or union.

SA/UR=HA Sudden separation through vulgarity and treachery. Losses through violence. To suddenly meet with an accident.

#
SA/UR=ZE Losses through conflagrations or acts of war. Enforced separation connected with losses.

UR/ZE=SA To obey a force with reluctance. Losses through explosions, also through violence; death through such. Sudden storm. Thunderstorms with damages.

SA/KR=UR Unexpected losses in securities.
UR/KR=SA Losses caused by the ruling powers, by powers suddenly breaking forth. To separate or rid oneself of something with help of the power of state.
#

SA/AP=UR The own original method. To prepare new ways. To isolate oneself. Astrological method. Sudden separation of many.
UR/AP=SA To be depressed and pessimistic through being disappointed. Expectations, hopes and wishes must be throttled. Disappointments, buried hopes. Failures in predicting events.
#
SA/UR=AD To get into or out of a tight spot by surprise. Sudden separation of a few.
SA/AD=UR Sudden jarring stop or throttling. To suddenly apply a brake. To suddenly change the course. Reverse the steering wheel.
UR/AD=SA Enmities which start suddenly and are of long duration. Hindrances and obstacles through the same. Sudden coming of death.
#

SA/VU=UR To become suddenly imprisoned or dependent.
UR/VU=SA To suddenly feel oneself extremely hindered. To suddenly experience the bitter taste of dependency.
#
SA/UR=PO Sudden shattering of confidence and faith. Sudden separation on a mental and spiritual plane. Division of opinion or conviction.
SA/PO=UR Surprising new knowledge in the field of education or leading others. To suddenly be shown as a model, which causes separation and hard feelings.
UR/PO=SA Expiring life or light. To give up the ghost, to breathe one's last. Death occurs.

SA/PL=NE A slow development seems to come to a standstill. One cannot see any change in a chronically slow development. A divorce extending over a long time.
NE/PL=SA A development which slowly leads to destruction.

NE/AP=SA Unclean methods of deceiving of many. Deception of or for many of long duration.

NE/AD=SA Slow change of conditions. The present conditions start slowly changing or preservation of a condition.
#
SA/NE=VU The force of habit.
SA/VU=NE To be imprisoned or dependent, not seeing the termination. To be imprisoned or dependent indefinitely.

PL/AP=SA Occurrences happening but once, which are without consequences, The expected consequences do not come forward.

PL/AD=SA Difficulty through divorce. Changes of existing conditions through separations.
#
SA/PL=VU To slowly get into mighty dependence.
SA/VU=PL To become imprisoned or dependent. The beginning of Imprisonment.

PL/ZE=AD To have to overcome the greatest difficulties at The start. Performance of duty under difficult or aggravating circumstances.
PL/AD=ZE To be forced to make up for lost time. To increase one's production or work under most trying and difficult circumstances. Compelled to get a lead over a person.
ZE/AD=PL The beginning of a change of conditions. Things keep going. Several changes before a definite settlement.
#
PL/ZE=VU To make mighty efforts. Great change in duties which have to be performed. Increase of duties.
PL/VU=ZE Situation improves due to great increase of accomplishments.
ZE/VU=PL The rise of great works (projects). The development of natural laws. Great transformations accomplished through natural powers.
#
PL/ZE=PO To develop a new way to see the world. To show the duties to be derived from such doctrine.
PL/PO=ZE To be in part leading (by inner conviction) in the development of a new outlook in life.
ZE/PO=PL Development of mental leadership. Development of one's own mental conviction.
#
PL/KR=AP From a tiny start developing great transformation. Many are examined, tested. A great change for many.
PL/AP=KR Great changes in the state, in politics, and in the government.
KR/AP=PL To develop a new science from a small beginning. Great and large changes and transformations. Increasing and expansion.
#
PL/KR=AD Transformation in a small circle. Deep reaching change for a few.
PL/AD=KR In the life of nations great changes in the existing forms of governments. In personal horoscopes, to live through such changes, or changed attitude of the native toward superiors, government, or the form of government.
KR/AD=PL The beginning and the development of an exclusive position. The development is connected either with change of authorities or other transformations.
#
PL/KR=VU To make oneself a top man. Mighty changes enforced.
PL/VU=KR Great development of the capabilities. Trial of these abilities. Government changes.
KR/VU=PL Changes and development of the form of government. Reorganization of the state. Changes and transformations in relation to great powers.
#
PL/KR=PO Highly mental or spiritual development. Mental or spiritual influence causes great changes. Great transformation in world concept.
PL/PO=KR An autocratic attitude in mental and emotional matters. An acknowledged leader as regards a concept or the meaning of life.
KR/PO=PL Cultural transformations, highly spiritual development.
#
PL/AP=AD Stagnation of development in a small circle. To be satisfied about it. Radical change for a few.
PL/AD=AP The change that brings happiness, contentment and is satisfactory.
AP/AD=PL The beginning of a development which takes a very satisfactory course.
#
PL/AP=VU Great transformation with extraordinary consequences for the future.
PL/VU=AP First indications of a later great change.
AP/VU=PL To prepare for a great success because of the way events are developing.
#
PL/AP=PO To experience radical transformations in the concept of the world with or together with friends, colleagues, comrades or like-minded people.
PL/PO=AP To exercise a transformation with many other people which is mental and emotional.
AP/PO=PL Development or beginning of a comradeship or an association of congenial people.
#
PL/AD=VU Mighty changes under grave hindrances.
PL/VU=AD The breakdown of a mighty development. Transformation or change caused by too great a resistance.
AD/VU=PL By fate-dictated development. Blows of fate which cause basic change, development.
#
PL/AD=PO Deep reaching changes in religion, in the mental and spiritual attitude and in the concept of the world.
PL/PO=AD A mental an emotional transformation in a small circle.
AD/PO=PL New ways of educating. Changes in educational conditions.
#
PL/VU=PO Great transformations of inclinations towards religion or world concept.
PL/PO=VU To experience a tremendous transformation in the mental as well as emotional attitude.
VU/PO=PL Developing a sense of honor, pride, self-esteem. A change in the concept of honor, pride and self-esteem.
#
CU/HA=ZE Poor family with many children. Blessed with many children. The same about nations. To create artistically with scant materials. To create with substitutes.
CU/ZE=HA Foundation of a concern dealing in minerals and coal. Destruction through nihilistic instincts. Disintegration of the existing social order.
HA/ZE=CU Destructive fire involving a large corporation, or a fire involving many people. Enmity of a corporation - to suffer from their antagonism.
#
CU/HA=KR Homes for children. Orphanages. Poorhouses.
CU/KR=HA Impoverished noble society. Misfortune of a great or big concern. Collapse of great corporations. Dormant art.
HA/KR=CU Mean deeds of a mob. Gang of criminals. Vulgar or obscene art.
#
CU/HA=AP Far spread affliction. Cognizance gained by joint sufferings. Improvised accommodation together with many. Study group for ancient sciences.
CU/AP=HA Obstacles caused by an artistic or scientific association or caused by a great organization. A tragedy which involves large circles.
HA/AP=CU Unpleasantness with the family or societies. Historic scientific society. Unpleasantness in connection with garbage, rags or other old material.
#
CU/HA=AD Grand public spirit under adverse conditions. To be avoided by others, proscribed (to prohibit, ban, outlaw). Raw material from used material. To stand together under sorrowful conditions.
CU/AD=HA The end or the beginning of a miserable marriage or partnership. Comradeship and partnership in grave and bad times. To live and to stick together in miserable circumstances.
HA/AD=CU A small association which suffers from different kinds of lack. Discord and meanness in partner ships and associations. Decrease of membership.
#
CU/HA=VU Concerted attacks to overcome poverty. Great obstacles to a community.
CU/VU=HA Great misfortune or tragedy in a family. Evil deeds of a mighty association. Great want in artistic accomplishment.
HA/VU=CU Oppressed or decimated families and great communities. The community that has been weakened through great losses or that is in constant danger. Gang of criminals.
#
CU/HA=PO Societies, where the standards are very low in reference to religion, or to mental or spiritual things or the outlook on life. Societies which linger in the past with their mentality.
CU/PO=HA Deterioration of sects. Associations which represent a certain philosophy of life or an ideology, on the stupidity and ignorance of their members. Spreading of erroneous beliefs or superstition.
HA/PO=CU To resent the masses. To be happy in special or exclusive circles. To be an adherent of odd ideological groups which are considered as fractional groups.
#
CU/ZE=KR Great creations in art. Great artistic creations in connection with others. Establishment of a company protected by the State. Comradeship. United troops. Army organization. Military organizations.
CU/KR=ZE Major accomplishments through cooperation. Orders and decorations from the highest authority. The art of mechanical engineering. Brilliant successes of the armi. The military union of several nations.
ZE/KR=CU Creator in art. To put new life into art. Creative art subsidized by the State.
#
CU/ZE=AP To unite for common efforts. Foundation of a corporation for trade or science.
CU/AP=ZE Family increase. Family-day, convention of the family. Artistic or scientific creations. To be with many in an arms carrying organization.
ZE/AP=CU Forming of great companies in science, commerce, trades or industry. Corporations, economic associations and study groups.
#
CU/ZE=AD Concerted efforts hit grave obstacles. Difficult beginning of a young firm.
CU/AD=ZE To tackle and work on a problem. Hindrances and delay in the new establishment of a firm. The start of a great artistic creation.
ZE/AD=CU Changes of conditions in the family as well as socially. Changes in the general social forms or manners.
#
CU/ZE=VU To unfold one's abilities and strength without hindrance. Opportunity for those who have the abilities and strength. Great artistic accomplishments.
CU/VU=ZE Union of great families through weddings or betrothals. A great organization concentrates all power on one point. People who accomplish things their own way. Great artistic products.
ZE/VU=CU Mighty efforts of a community. To manage and direct the forces of a company, of a community etc. To appear as the leader. Great artistic creations.
#
CU/ZE=PO Founding of a community based on the same conviction. Artistic creation on a mental plane. Common efforts inspired by religion and outlook on life.
CU/PO=ZE To take part in the leadership and the institution of a religious society. To be the co-founder.
ZE/PO=CU Mental attitude or behavior of a society or a family. To hold or to take the leadership in some field of art. Creative mental accomplishment in the field of art.
#
CU/KR=AP Great distinguished social gatherings. Extensive artistic or scientific associations. To be or to become known to many through great art.
CU/AP=KR Authority in an artistic or scientific field. To receive favors from the boss, the chief, the state or head of family on account of artistic or scientific accomplishments.
KR/AP=CU The great artist. Great expansion of a community or society. Great peace movement. Reconciliation. Prosperity of all peaceful matters. Dealing with art products, Important art.
#
CU/KR=AD Clumsy behavior and hermit-like life. To be noted unfavorably through dull manners which cause disadvantages and losses. To exclude oneself from a social group. To be socially banned. To make oneself impossible.
CU/AD=KR An association dissolved by the state or the authorities. The State causes obstacles and difficulties during the dissolution of a corporation. Dissolution or breaking up of treaties, of the constitution, or form of government.
KR/AD=CU To hold an exclusive position in art, in society, or in the family. Activities in an unusual art, an unusual artist.
#
CU/KR=VU Might and influence through personal appearance or the distinguished social background to which one belongs. To be very dominant or domineering in art. Great state enterprises.
CU/VU=KR A society sanctioned by the State. Ingenious artistic accomplishment. Privileged class, to belong to the nobility.
KR/VU=CU Associations protected and subsidized by the state. United great powers.
#
CU/KR=PO State protected religious congregations.
CU/PO=KR To accept the leadership and management of a religious organization. The state subsidized and managed religious sect. The cuts of the state.
KR/PO=CU Community with the same spiritual and cultural attitude. Cultural and religions congregations. Political party.
#
CU/AP=AD To be noticed in a small circle for artistic or scientific accomplishments. The possibilities in such fields are hindered.
CU/AD=AP Satisfied with a community of a few. A small scientific society. Co-operation in a small circle.
AP/AD=CU To be satisfied with the community in which one lives. Satisfaction over social relation or conditions. The contented artist.
#
CU/AP=VU Family meetings. Great family festivities or celebrations. Greatest success through artistic or scientific accomplishments. The great economical success of a firm. Conference of an organization.
CU/VU=AP Expansion of a mighty association. Greatly extended art creations. Art products for the masses.
AP/VU=CU Strongest success in endeavors with others. Great social prosperity. Great artistic success.
#
CU/AP=PO To be joined with others of the same faith. Artistic or scientific union by the same religion or world outlook.
CU/PO=AP The spreading of a religious cult, the joining up of people of the same faith.
AP/PO=CU Congeniality in a family or in a community. Same mentality caused by the same social conditions. The same concept of art.
#
CU/AD=VU To experience great obstacles and hindrances through the family or partner. To feel or be suppressed through such conditions. To fight or struggle hard for an artistic creation. Often to be forced to fight.
CU/VU=AD To go to the bottom of things, to the source of things. To be alone (single) at a mighty artistic creation, under pressure. Combating great obstacles, under pressure.
AD/VU=CU To be put in a difficult state or hard trial by or with an association. Artists experience great hindrances.
#
CU/AD=PO To differ from the opinion of a community in mental, psychic and philosophical respect, to feel lonely among others for this reason.
CU/PO=AD Knowledge through the faith. To quit a religious faith, to stay outside of the faith, no inclinations to join. Disadvantages and oppression through it.
AD/PO=CU Community's ideas on educational ideals. Alone in one's educational ideals. Private teacher. Dissolution of an educational association.
#
CU/VU=PO To exercise enormous influence in the mental and spiritual as well as the philosophical sphere. Mighty creations in the mental and spiritual field of science.
CU/PO=VU The power of influence through faith. Power in or through a religious society.
VU/PO=CU The honor of a community, family, or social stratum. The pride and self-esteem of a class of people.
#
HA/ZE=KR Miserable serving conditions, caused by mismanagement by the government. Expanded unemployment. Dissatisfaction against the existing conditions in the government. Big destructive fire.
HA/KR=ZE Brutality and meanness well aimed, or directed. Meanness born out of hate. To start a large destructive fire. To order war atrocities.
ZE/KR=HA Great efforts under trying and difficult circumstances. Overworking. Surprising and grave events in war. Fire catastrophes. Ordered war atrocities.
#
HA/ZE=AP Spreading of a hateful and ugly way of thinking. Common hate of many. Expansion of an industry. Increase of coal production.
HA/AP=ZE Mass misery and large destruction caused by war hate. Mass destruction. Creative activity in the historical sciences.
ZE/AP=HA To be faced with one's past. Retribution for one's past evil deeds. Dealing with rags and coal. To study the past.
#
HA/ZE=AD Trying to find recreation together with few. Small groups fighting each other. Coal mines.
HA/AD=ZE To relieve or get 6d of poor living conditions or lack of raw materials through great energetic efforts. To go ahead with a fanatical eagerness while carrying on a hateful fight against the enemy.
ZE/AD=HA To find the existing conditions repulsive and disgusting. Forced to be part of creating disgusting things.
#
HA/ZE=VU Decrease of activity. Mighty efforts to do away with unemployment. Increase of lowness and vulgarity.
HA/VU=ZE Meanness, malignancy and evil deeds born out of hate or a weak will.
ZE/VU=HA Mighty efforts under difficult circumstances and ugly conditions. Filth and disorder. Intense hate and resentment.
#
HA/ZE=PO Want of intellectual endeavors. Indifferent in mental sphere.
HA/PO=ZE Through isolation or separation to become the target of hate and spitefulness.
ZE/PO=HA Ugly mental attitude and behavior. Miserable disposition. Lack of character. Poor report card.
#
HA/KR=AP Grand scale meanness. To detect large corruption. Great lack in the government becomes known to the public.
HA/AP=KR Misery of the masses brought on by incompetence of the government. To be an authority in historical science. Abilities in the field of magic and occult science.
KR/AP=HA Spreading of lack and poverty. Great meanness and villainess. Celebrities in the field of sciences of antiquity.
#
HA/KR=AD Great mean acts committed in a limited scope. Mean acts stay hidden. Evil deeds or acts which are not discovered.
HA/AD=KR Great negligence. Great lack of raw materials. Meanness and villainess which is unsurpassed hitherto. Strange methods of the government.
KR/AD=HA Odd and difficult to detect evil deeds and concealed meanness.
#
HA/KR=VU Great brutality, meanness and evil. The government proceeds in a devilish way. Murders on a large scale.
HA/VU=KR Gigantic crimes and evil deeds or meanness. Heavy oppression and suppression by governments and ruling powers.
KR/VU=HA The power of the state does not stop short of anything. Great injustice caused by great powers, great misery and misfortune. Great murder caused by war.
#
HA/KR=PO Base or mediocre way of seeing life and the world. Wickedness, lies or distortion in the field of ideology or world outlook.
HA/PO=KR To be subjected to great meanness and losses, particularly by the authorities or higher ups because of one's special position.
KR/PO=HA The spiritual or cultural attitude of base people. Low kind of cultural endeavor. Cultures of the past.
#
HA/AP=AD Poverty and need of a few. To be part of these few.
HA/AD=AP Beginning of hardship for many people. Satisfactory revelations in history science.
AP/AD=HA Discontentment, worries, sorrow through lack and need.
#
HA/AP=VU Massive spreading of poverty and need. Mighty misery. Great destruction. Greatest success in trading with scrap or in the science of history. Great success in secret science or magic.
HA/VU=AP Spread and extended wickedness and evil deeds. Failure in industry commerce and trade. Success in history sciences.
AP/VU=HA To be unsuccessful, defeats and failures. Must accept a bad break or blow of fate. To be defenseless against the evil deeds of others.
#
HA/AP=PO Share and share alike. To establish connections with people who are like minded in the social and political respect. The view of the world corresponds with the social situation. To find adherents and like minded people for a science.
HA/PO=AP To separate or isolate from many or to have a special position among many.
AP/PO=HA Wanting in comradeship. To be disgusted with colleagues comrades or people of the same destiny. To lead a separate life among comrades and colleagues. To withdraw from them.
#
HA/AD=VU To overdo frugality. To be under heavy pressure through need, starvation and enmity.
HA/VU=AD Wickedness and evil acts in a limited scope.
AD/VU=HA To get into a period of need and privation through hard blows of fate.
#
HA/AD=PO Ignorance and mental indifference. To occupy one's mind with remote antiquity. To be interested in prehistoric things or times. To occupy one's mind with long-past times.
HA/PO=AD To hold a special position among a few. To be outstanding.
AD/PO=HA Defects in education and training.
#
HA/VU=PO To have a guilty conscience. To be depressed through ruling world outlook. Meditation on the past.
HA/PO=VU To be conscious of one's isolated and exceptional position. To become very noted or notorious. To experience refusal and rejection in a special position.
VU/PO=HA To have a lack of self-confidence, pride, honor and dignity. That which is despised, undignified or dishonorable.
#
ZE/KR=AP Eager to learn, to gain knowledge. Many are in competition. Great enterprises and efforts in science, commerce, trades and industry. Expansion of armament or war.
ZE/AP=KR To be outstanding through one's eagerness for knowledge, study and research. To leave others behind, to be without competition. To surpass the competitors in science or economy.
KR/AP=ZE Enlarging of the field of operation. Expanding of the economic life. New branches in economic life. To specialize in science.
#
ZE/KR=AD Great creation on a limited scope. Competition among a few. To get a special commission. To be outstanding through special accomplishments. To appear as an odd character through the behavior.
ZE/AD=KR To start something great. Changed conditions brought on by the government.
KR/AD=ZE Difficult beginning of a great enterprise within narrow bounds. Special order. Special commission or position during war.
#
ZE/KR=VU The use of much violent force. Blind force. Tremendous forces are engaged. The outbreak of a great war. Great powers locked in battle.
ZE/VU=KR To gain authority through great accomplishments. Great powers locked in battle. Great efforts of a government. Great power efforts. Measures by force.
KR/VU=ZE To make good (carry out) through great efforts. The governmental power, ruthlessly applied, has its way.
#
ZE/KR=PO Great mental struggles. A struggle for one's convictions. To back one's convictions or way of seeing the world and life. To be tested to the utmost.
ZE/PO=KR Great mental accomplishment. To be an authority because of ability and mental creation. Mental leadership and direction.
KR/PO=ZE Highly mental creation. To have one's mental ability tested. To take the lead in highly mental things, to be a leader. Great cultural accomplishment.
#
ZE/AP=AD To terminate an apprenticeship, a study or research work. Deep research work in a narrowed field. Suspended economics.
ZE/AD=AP Large changes of conditions. Many are forced to begin anew.
AP/AD=ZE To gain a feeling of satisfaction through the own work.
#
ZE/AP=VU To be loaded with eagerness for learning, studying or research. Strongest economic success.
ZE/VU=AP Mighty expansion and success of peaceful efforts. Successful efforts in science, commerce, trades and industry.
AP/VU=ZE To overcome a depression. To struggle successfully to the top.
#
ZE/AP=PO Learning, studying and research work in the spiritual and mental realm. People of the same caliber are associates. In the economic respect persons are equal. Members of a guild (such as craft guild). A party-spirit.
ZE/PO=AP To be a leader for many, especially for coworkers. Creative accomplishment in science and commerce.
AP/PO=ZE To be a leader through one's activity with similar minded people.
#
ZE/AD=VU Abrupt beginning of new work. To start with a mighty effort. Great forces are concentrated toward one goal. Great difficulties in the initial state. Long held-back forces release themselves violently. Volcanic eruption.
ZE/VU=AD A great enterprise is started on a small scale with expedients. To lay the foundation for a great work.
AD/VU=ZE Hindered in work by obstacles, a difficult beginning is forcibly aggravated by others.
#
ZE/AD=PO To start something new in intellectual respect. To demonstrate ones abilities and development in a test. To meet with changed conditions in the intellectual and ideological respect.
ZE/PO=AD A creative mind that can see very deeply. To be noticed in a small circle right at the start for accomplishment.
AD/PO=ZE Enforced education and training. Tests, examinations. Trained in the use of arms.
#
ZE/VU=PO Potent intellectual or spiritual work, great creations of this kind. Conclusive evidence. Demonstrated mental faculties. Impressive proofs. Powerful documents.
ZE/PO=VU Mighty mental work. A very great mental or spiritual leader, Tremendous mental compulsion. Proof beyond a doubt.
VU/PO=ZE To practice self-control and discipline. To show pride, honor, dignity. To be an example of these fine qualities. Military pride.
#
KR/AP=AD The specialist. Authority in a special field. Authority for a few in a small circle. Dealing with special products.
KR/AD=AP Many must lead an odd live, under unusual circumstances.
AP/AD=KR Without care and worry. To be very satisfied with one's special mode of life. Best expression for this formula is "'Sanssouci.".
#
KR/AP=VU The mighty recognized leader in science, commerce or trade. The great scientific success.
KR/VU=AP To be a mighty authority for many. Great efforts of a state in peaceful fields. The peaceful great power.
AP/VU=KR Outstanding capabilities. Through ability to success. Extraordinary prosperity. Unusual successes.
#
KR/AP=PO Recognized authority among people who are scientifically and ideologically of the same opinion. Great scientific perceptions.
KR/PO=AP To be the wise man for many. General enlightenment and widely spread culture. Great knowledge and wisdom together. Culture as the force of destiny, through the same cultural attitude joined. The science of culture.
AP/PO=KR To be outstandingly cultured among cultured people. To be rated as an example.
#
KR/AD=VU To create a sensation as an odd character. To call attention to oneself. In this position one must count on grave obstacles.
KR/VU=AD Past and out-of-date forms of a state government. Isolation of a great power. States which are considered peculiar by other states. The end of the power of a state. The dead or suspended state.
AD/VU=KR To be noted through something which is extraordinary. To draw attention through unusual events and odd or peculiar fateful occurrences.
#
KR/AD=PO To build one's religion and view of life on firm ground one has to search deeply.
KR/PO=AD Highly spiritual and cultural endeavors intended for a few only. The spiritual and cultural elite.
AD/PO=KR To be subjected to a special education or training. Specially gifted as trainer or teacher.
#
KR/VU=PO Great spiritual influence. Might in spiritual fields or through spiritual influence. Great powers through religion, spirituality or through the outlook on life.
KR/PO=VU The great man who is near God. Tremendous spirituality. A great outstanding culture.
VU/PO=KR To possess an elevated position of honor and esteem. To be conscious of one's own value. Great self-confidence. Dignitaries.
#
AP/AD=VU 'Pride goeth before a fall.' To be arrogant and presumptuous.
AP/VU=AD Strongest success on a small scale.
AD/VU=AP To be exposed with many to a blow of fate.
#
AP/AD=PO To feel safe at home through religion and outlook on life. Contentment through being in high minded company and with people of similar destiny and outlook on life.
AP/PO=AD Great conviction and steadfastness. Exclusive circle of similar minded people.
AD/PO=AP Educated and trained With many. Satisfactory state of education or training.
#
AP/VU=PO Great success in the mental realm. Successful religion or philosophical conception of the world. Spreading of a religion or philosophy of life.
AP/PO=VU To gain great success through colleagues and comrades. To be noted or to create a sensation among them.
VU/PO=AP To be held in a high position of esteem by many other people. To enjoy success and honor because one is held in an elevated position of tremendous respect by others.
#
AD/VU=PO To be strongly limited and subjected to hindrances and handicaps by fate with regard to mental or intellectual education, development and culture. Mental bondage. To be under guardianship.
AD/PO=VU Mighty and lasting influence of education. To be affected that way, or to affect others that way.
VU/PO=AD To enjoy esteem and respect in a small circle.
